========== 文件：apps\api\app\database.py ==========
import os
from pathlib import Path
from importlib import import_module

from sqlalchemy import create_engine
from sqlalchemy.orm import declarative_base, sessionmaker

Base = declarative_base()

APP_ROOT = Path(__file__).resolve().parents[3]
DATA_ROOT = Path(os.environ.get("APP_DATA_DIR", str(APP_ROOT)))
ROOT_DIR = DATA_ROOT
STORAGE_DIR = DATA_ROOT / "storage"
STORAGE_DIR.mkdir(parents=True, exist_ok=True)

DATABASE_PATH = STORAGE_DIR / "ai_comic_studio.db"

engine = create_engine(
    f"sqlite:///{DATABASE_PATH.as_posix()}",
    connect_args={"check_same_thread": False},
    future=True
)

SessionLocal = sessionmaker(
    autocommit=False,
    autoflush=False,
    bind=engine
)


def get_db():
    db = SessionLocal()
    try:
        yield db
    finally:
        db.close()


MODEL_MODULES = [
    "app.models.novel",
    "app.models.studio",
    "app.models.visual",
    "app.models.story",
    "app.models.media",
    "app.models.editing",
]


def init_database():
    print("正在注册数据库模型……")

    for module_name in MODEL_MODULES:
        try:
            import_module(module_name)
            print(f"[模型已注册] {module_name}")
        except ModuleNotFoundError as exc:
            if exc.name == module_name:
                print(f"[跳过不存在的模型] {module_name}")
            else:
                print(f"[模型依赖错误] {module_name}: {exc}")
                raise

    Base.metadata.create_all(bind=engine)
    print(f"[数据库初始化完成] {DATABASE_PATH}")

    return True



========== 文件：apps\api\app\main.py ==========
import importlib
import logging

from fastapi import FastAPI
from fastapi.middleware.cors import CORSMiddleware

from app.database import init_database

logging.basicConfig(level=logging.INFO)
logger = logging.getLogger("ai_comic_studio")

app = FastAPI(
    title="AI漫剧制作大师 API",
    description="小说改编、AI创作、视觉素材、分镜、视频生产与剪辑",
    version="1.0.0"
)

app.add_middleware(
    CORSMiddleware,
    allow_origins=[
        "http://localhost:3000",
        "http://127.0.0.1:3000",
        "http://localhost:3001",
        "http://127.0.0.1:3001",
    ],
    allow_credentials=True,
    allow_methods=["*"],
    allow_headers=["*"],
)

ROUTER_MODULES = [
    "app.routers.novels",
    "app.routers.studio",
    "app.routers.visual",
    "app.routers.story",
    "app.routers.media",
    "app.routers.video_editor",
]

_loaded_routers = []


def register_routers():
    for module_name in ROUTER_MODULES:
        try:
            module = importlib.import_module(module_name)
            router = getattr(module, "router", None)

            if router is None:
                router = getattr(module, "api_router", None)

            if router is None:
                logger.warning("模块没有找到 router：%s", module_name)
                continue

            app.include_router(router)
            _loaded_routers.append(module_name)
            logger.info("路由注册成功：%s", module_name)

        except ModuleNotFoundError as exc:
            if exc.name == module_name:
                logger.warning("路由模块不存在，跳过：%s", module_name)
            else:
                logger.exception("路由依赖导入失败：%s", module_name)

        except Exception:
            logger.exception("路由加载失败：%s", module_name)


register_routers()


@app.on_event("startup")
def startup_event():
    init_database()
    logger.info("AI漫剧制作大师 API 已启动")


@app.get("/")
def root():
    return {
        "application": "AI漫剧制作大师",
        "status": "running",
        "version": "1.0.0"
    }


@app.get("/health")
def health():
    return {
        "status": "ok",
        "database": "initialized",
        "loaded_routers": _loaded_routers
    }


@app.get("/api/system/modules")
def system_modules():
    return {
        "loaded_routers": _loaded_routers,
        "available_routers": ROUTER_MODULES
    }

========== 文件：apps\api\app\models\novel.py ==========
from datetime import datetime
from sqlalchemy import (
    DateTime, ForeignKey, Integer, String, Text, JSON, Boolean
)
from sqlalchemy.orm import Mapped, mapped_column, relationship
from app.database import Base

class Novel(Base):
    __tablename__ = "novels"

    id: Mapped[int] = mapped_column(Integer, primary_key=True, index=True)
    title: Mapped[str] = mapped_column(String(200), nullable=False)
    description: Mapped[str] = mapped_column(Text, default="")
    genre: Mapped[str] = mapped_column(String(100), default="未分类")
    source_text: Mapped[str] = mapped_column(Text, default="")
    status: Mapped[str] = mapped_column(String(50), default="创作中")
    created_at: Mapped[datetime] = mapped_column(DateTime, default=datetime.utcnow)
    updated_at: Mapped[datetime] = mapped_column(
        DateTime, default=datetime.utcnow, onupdate=datetime.utcnow
    )

    chapters = relationship(
        "Chapter", back_populates="novel",
        cascade="all, delete-orphan", order_by="Chapter.chapter_number"
    )
    analysis_items = relationship(
        "AnalysisItem", back_populates="novel",
        cascade="all, delete-orphan"
    )

class Chapter(Base):
    __tablename__ = "chapters"

    id: Mapped[int] = mapped_column(Integer, primary_key=True, index=True)
    novel_id: Mapped[int] = mapped_column(
        ForeignKey("novels.id", ondelete="CASCADE"), index=True
    )
    chapter_number: Mapped[int] = mapped_column(Integer, default=1)
    title: Mapped[str] = mapped_column(String(300), default="未命名章节")
    content: Mapped[str] = mapped_column(Text, default="")
    word_count: Mapped[int] = mapped_column(Integer, default=0)
    created_at: Mapped[datetime] = mapped_column(DateTime, default=datetime.utcnow)
    updated_at: Mapped[datetime] = mapped_column(
        DateTime, default=datetime.utcnow, onupdate=datetime.utcnow
    )

    novel = relationship("Novel", back_populates="chapters")

class AnalysisItem(Base):
    __tablename__ = "analysis_items"

    id: Mapped[int] = mapped_column(Integer, primary_key=True, index=True)
    novel_id: Mapped[int] = mapped_column(
        ForeignKey("novels.id", ondelete="CASCADE"), index=True
    )
    category: Mapped[str] = mapped_column(String(50), index=True)
    name: Mapped[str] = mapped_column(String(300))
    description: Mapped[str] = mapped_column(Text, default="")
    data: Mapped[dict] = mapped_column(JSON, default=dict)
    confirmed: Mapped[bool] = mapped_column(Boolean, default=False)
    created_at: Mapped[datetime] = mapped_column(DateTime, default=datetime.utcnow)

    novel = relationship("Novel", back_populates="analysis_items")

========== 文件：apps\api\app\routers\media.py ==========
import os
import uuid
import json
import re
from pathlib import Path
from typing import Optional

from fastapi import APIRouter, Depends, HTTPException, UploadFile, File
from pydantic import BaseModel, Field
from sqlalchemy.orm import Session

from app.database import get_db
from app.models.media import MediaTask, MediaFile, SubtitleTrack
from app.models.studio import AIProviderConfig, AIRuntimeSetting
from app.services.media_service import (
    ROOT, STORAGE, VIDEO_DIR, AUDIO_DIR, SUBTITLE_DIR,
    decrypt_api_key, http_json, http_download,
    safe_storage_file, run_ffmpeg, probe_media
)

router = APIRouter(prefix="/api/media", tags=["媒体生产"])


def get_provider(db, provider_name, confirm_paid=False):
    config = db.query(AIProviderConfig).filter(
        AIProviderConfig.provider == provider_name
    ).first()
    if not config:
        raise HTTPException(404, f"AI 服务商 {provider_name} 尚未配置")

    if not getattr(config, "enabled", False):
        raise HTTPException(400, "该 AI 服务商尚未启用")

    runtime = db.query(AIRuntimeSetting).first()
    if not runtime or not getattr(runtime, "network_enabled", False):
        raise HTTPException(403, "全局网络调用开关已关闭")

    paid = bool(getattr(config, "paid", True))
    if paid:
        if not getattr(runtime, "paid_api_allowed", False):
            raise HTTPException(403, "付费 API 总授权未开启")
        if not confirm_paid:
            raise HTTPException(403, "此操作可能产生费用，必须明确确认后才能调用")

    encrypted = (
        getattr(config, "api_key_encrypted", None)
        or getattr(config, "encrypted_api_key", None)
    )
    if not encrypted:
        raise HTTPException(400, "该服务商尚未保存 API Key")

    base_url = getattr(config, "base_url", None)
    if not base_url:
        raise HTTPException(400, "该服务商尚未设置 API Base URL")

    try:
        api_key = decrypt_api_key(encrypted)
    except Exception as exc:
        raise HTTPException(500, str(exc))

    return config, api_key, base_url.rstrip("/")


def value_at(data, dotted_path):
    current = data
    for part in dotted_path.split("."):
        if isinstance(current, dict):
            current = current.get(part)
        elif isinstance(current, list) and part.isdigit():
            current = current[int(part)]
        else:
            return None
        if current is None:
            return None
    return current


class VideoTaskInput(BaseModel):
    provider: str
    model: str
    prompt: str = Field(min_length=1, max_length=20000)
    duration: int = Field(default=10, ge=1, le=600)
    aspect_ratio: str = "16:9"
    confirm_paid: bool = False

    # 每家视频服务商的 API 路径和请求结构可能不同，允许配置
    create_path: str = "/videos/generations"
    status_path_template: str = "/videos/{task_id}"
    task_id_field: str = "id"
    video_url_field: str = "video_url"
    status_field: str = "status"
    extra_payload: dict = {}


@router.post("/video/tasks")
def create_video_task(data: VideoTaskInput, db: Session = Depends(get_db)):
    config, key, base = get_provider(db, data.provider, data.confirm_paid)

    payload = {
        "model": data.model,
        "prompt": data.prompt,
        "duration": data.duration,
        "aspect_ratio": data.aspect_ratio,
        **data.extra_payload
    }
    path = "/" + data.create_path.lstrip("/")
    try:
        result = http_json(base + path, key, body=payload, timeout=300)
        remote_id = value_at(result, data.task_id_field)
        if not remote_id:
            raise RuntimeError(
                "视频服务商没有返回任务编号。请按该服务商文档配置请求路径和返回字段。"
            )

        task = MediaTask(
            task_type="video",
            provider=data.provider,
            model=data.model,
            status="submitted",
            prompt=data.prompt,
            remote_task_id=str(remote_id),
            request_data={
                "base_url": base,
                "status_path_template": data.status_path_template,
                "video_url_field": data.video_url_field,
                "status_field": data.status_field,
                "task_id_field": data.task_id_field,
                "confirm_paid": data.confirm_paid
            },
            result_data=result
        )
        db.add(task)
        db.commit()
        db.refresh(task)
        return {"task_id": task.id, "remote_task_id": remote_id,
                "status": task.status, "provider_response": result}
    except Exception as exc:
        raise HTTPException(502, f"视频生成请求失败：{exc}")


@router.post("/video/tasks/{task_id}/refresh")
def refresh_video_task(task_id: int, db: Session = Depends(get_db)):
    task = db.query(MediaTask).filter(MediaTask.id == task_id).first()
    if not task or task.task_type != "video":
        raise HTTPException(404, "视频任务不存在")

    config, key, base = get_provider(
        db, task.provider, bool((task.request_data or {}).get("confirm_paid"))
    )
    settings = task.request_data or {}
    template = settings.get("status_path_template", "/videos/{task_id}")
    path = template.replace("{task_id}", str(task.remote_task_id))
    try:
        result = http_json(base + "/" + path.lstrip("/"), key, "GET")
        status = str(value_at(result, settings.get("status_field", "status")) or "processing")
        task.result_data = result
        task.status = status

        video_url = value_at(result, settings.get("video_url_field", "video_url"))
        if video_url:
            filename = f"video_{task.id}_{uuid.uuid4().hex[:8]}.mp4"
            destination = VIDEO_DIR / filename
            http_download(str(video_url), destination)
            media = MediaFile(
                name=filename, category="video", file_path=str(destination),
                mime_type="video/mp4", file_size=destination.stat().st_size
            )
            db.add(media)
            task.result_path = str(destination)
            task.status = "completed"

        db.commit()
        return {"task_id": task.id, "status": task.status,
                "result_path": task.result_path, "provider_response": result}
    except Exception as exc:
        task.error = str(exc)
        db.commit()
        raise HTTPException(502, f"查询视频任务失败：{exc}")


@router.get("/video/tasks")
def list_video_tasks(db: Session = Depends(get_db)):
    tasks = db.query(MediaTask).filter(
        MediaTask.task_type == "video"
    ).order_by(MediaTask.id.desc()).limit(200).all()
    return [{
        "id": t.id, "provider": t.provider, "model": t.model,
        "status": t.status, "prompt": t.prompt,
        "remote_task_id": t.remote_task_id,
        "result_path": t.result_path, "error": t.error
    } for t in tasks]


class SpeechInput(BaseModel):
    provider: str
    model: str
    text: str = Field(min_length=1, max_length=4096)
    voice: str = "alloy"
    instructions: Optional[str] = None
    response_format: str = "mp3"
    speed: float = Field(default=1.0, ge=0.25, le=4.0)
    confirm_paid: bool = False


@router.post("/speech/generate")
def generate_speech(data: SpeechInput, db: Session = Depends(get_db)):
    config, key, base = get_provider(db, data.provider, data.confirm_paid)

    payload = {
        "model": data.model,
        "input": data.text,
        "voice": data.voice,
        "response_format": data.response_format,
        "speed": data.speed
    }
    if data.instructions:
        payload["instructions"] = data.instructions

    filename = f"speech_{uuid.uuid4().hex}.{data.response_format}"
    destination = AUDIO_DIR / filename
    import urllib.request
    import urllib.error

    request = urllib.request.Request(
        base + "/audio/speech",
        data=json.dumps(payload, ensure_ascii=False).encode("utf-8"),
        headers={
            "Authorization": f"Bearer {key}",
            "Content-Type": "application/json"
        },
        method="POST"
    )
    try:
        with urllib.request.urlopen(request, timeout=300) as response:
            with open(destination, "wb") as output:
                while True:
                    chunk = response.read(1024 * 1024)
                    if not chunk:
                        break
                    output.write(chunk)

        media = MediaFile(
            name=filename, category="audio", file_path=str(destination),
            mime_type="audio/" + data.response_format,
            file_size=destination.stat().st_size
        )
        task = MediaTask(
            task_type="speech", provider=data.provider, model=data.model,
            status="completed", prompt=data.text, result_path=str(destination)
        )
        db.add(media)
        db.add(task)
        db.commit()
        return {"status": "completed", "file": filename,
                "path": str(destination), "size": destination.stat().st_size}
    except urllib.error.HTTPError as exc:
        detail = exc.read().decode("utf-8", errors="replace")
        raise HTTPException(502, f"语音 API 返回 HTTP {exc.code}: {detail[:1500]}")
    except Exception as exc:
        raise HTTPException(502, f"语音生成失败：{exc}")


@router.post("/files/upload")
async def upload_media(file: UploadFile = File(...), category: str = "video",
                       db: Session = Depends(get_db)):
    allowed = {"video": VIDEO_DIR, "audio": AUDIO_DIR, "image": STORAGE / "images"}
    if category not in allowed:
        raise HTTPException(400, "不支持的素材类别")

    folder = allowed[category]
    folder.mkdir(parents=True, exist_ok=True)
    suffix = Path(file.filename or "upload.bin").suffix[:20]
    filename = f"{uuid.uuid4().hex}{suffix}"
    destination = folder / filename
    size = 0
    limit = 2 * 1024 * 1024 * 1024

    try:
        with open(destination, "wb") as output:
            while True:
                chunk = await file.read(1024 * 1024)
                if not chunk:
                    break
                size += len(chunk)
                if size > limit:
                    raise HTTPException(413, "单个文件不能超过 2GB")
                output.write(chunk)

        media = MediaFile(
            name=file.filename or filename, category=category,
            file_path=str(destination), mime_type=file.content_type,
            file_size=size
        )
        db.add(media)
        db.commit()
        db.refresh(media)
        return {"id": media.id, "name": media.name, "category": category,
                "size": size, "path": media.file_path}
    except Exception:
        if destination.exists():
            destination.unlink()
        raise


@router.get("/files")
def list_media_files(category: Optional[str] = None, db: Session = Depends(get_db)):
    query = db.query(MediaFile)
    if category:
        query = query.filter(MediaFile.category == category)
    return [{
        "id": f.id, "name": f.name, "category": f.category,
        "path": f.file_path, "size": f.file_size,
        "mime_type": f.mime_type, "created_at": f.created_at
    } for f in query.order_by(MediaFile.id.desc()).limit(500).all()]


class SubtitleCue(BaseModel):
    start: float = Field(ge=0)
    end: float = Field(gt=0)
    text: str = Field(min_length=1, max_length=5000)


class SubtitleInput(BaseModel):
    name: str = "字幕"
    cues: list[SubtitleCue]


def srt_time(seconds):
    ms = int(round(seconds * 1000))
    hours, ms = divmod(ms, 3600000)
    minutes, ms = divmod(ms, 60000)
    secs, ms = divmod(ms, 1000)
    return f"{hours:02}:{minutes:02}:{secs:02},{ms:03}"


@router.post("/subtitles")
def create_subtitle(data: SubtitleInput, db: Session = Depends(get_db)):
    if not data.cues:
        raise HTTPException(400, "字幕内容不能为空")
    lines = []
    for index, cue in enumerate(data.cues, 1):
        if cue.end <= cue.start:
            raise HTTPException(400, f"第 {index} 条字幕结束时间必须大于开始时间")
        lines.extend([
            str(index),
            f"{srt_time(cue.start)} --> {srt_time(cue.end)}",
            cue.text,
            ""
        ])
    filename = f"subtitle_{uuid.uuid4().hex}.srt"
    destination = SUBTITLE_DIR / filename
    destination.write_text("\n".join(lines), encoding="utf-8-sig")

    track = SubtitleTrack(name=data.name, file_path=str(destination), format="srt")
    db.add(track)
    db.commit()
    db.refresh(track)
    return {"id": track.id, "name": track.name, "path": track.file_path}


@router.get("/subtitles")
def list_subtitles(db: Session = Depends(get_db)):
    rows = db.query(SubtitleTrack).order_by(SubtitleTrack.id.desc()).all()
    return [{"id": x.id, "name": x.name, "path": x.file_path,
             "format": x.format} for x in rows]


class RenderInput(BaseModel):
    video_path: str
    output_name: str = "rendered_video.mp4"
    audio_path: Optional[str] = None
    subtitle_path: Optional[str] = None
    music_volume: float = Field(default=0.25, ge=0, le=2)
    original_volume: float = Field(default=1.0, ge=0, le=2)
    width: int = 1920
    height: int = 1080


@router.post("/render")
def render_video(data: RenderInput):
    try:
        video = safe_storage_file(data.video_path)
        output_name = re.sub(r"[^a-zA-Z0-9_\-\u4e00-\u9fff]", "_", data.output_name)
        if not output_name.lower().endswith(".mp4"):
            output_name += ".mp4"
        output = VIDEO_DIR / f"{uuid.uuid4().hex}_{output_name}"

        args = ["-i", str(video)]
        audio = safe_storage_file(data.audio_path) if data.audio_path else None
        subtitle = safe_storage_file(data.subtitle_path) if data.subtitle_path else None

        if audio:
            args += ["-i", str(audio)]
            filter_complex = (
                f"[0:a]volume={data.original_volume}[original];"
                f"[1:a]volume={data.music_volume}[music];"
                "[original][music]amix=inputs=2:duration=first:dropout_transition=2[mix]"
            )
            args += ["-filter_complex", filter_complex, "-map", "0:v:0",
                     "-map", "[mix]", "-shortest"]
        else:
            args += ["-map", "0:v:0", "-map", "0:a?"]

        if subtitle:
            escaped = str(subtitle).replace("\\", "/").replace(":", "\\:")
            args += ["-vf", f"subtitles='{escaped}'"]

        args += [
            "-vf" if not subtitle else "-metadata", 
        ] if False else []

        args += [
            "-c:v", "libx264", "-preset", "medium", "-crf", "20",
            "-c:a", "aac", "-movflags", "+faststart", str(output)
        ]
        run_ffmpeg(args)
        return {"status": "completed", "output": str(output),
                "size": output.stat().st_size}
    except Exception as exc:
        raise HTTPException(500, f"视频合成失败：{exc}")


@router.get("/probe")
def media_probe(path: str):
    try:
        file_path = safe_storage_file(path)
        return probe_media(file_path)
    except Exception as exc:
        raise HTTPException(400, str(exc))

        "ffmpeg": shutil.which(os.environ.get("FFMPEG_PATH", "ffmpeg")),
        "ffprobe": shutil.which(os.environ.get("FFPROBE_PATH", "ffprobe")),
        "storage": str(STORAGE)
    }

class NovelCreate(BaseModel):
    title: str = Field(min_length=1, max_length=200)
    description: str = ""
    genre: str = "未分类"

class NovelUpdate(BaseModel):
    title: str | None = None
    description: str | None = None
    genre: str | None = None
    status: str | None = None

class ChapterUpdate(BaseModel):
    title: str | None = None
    content: str | None = None

class AnalysisUpdate(BaseModel):
    name: str | None = None

def novel_dict(item):
    return {
        "id": item.id,
        "title": item.title,
        "description": item.description,
        "genre": item.genre,
        "status": item.status,
        "chapter_count": len(item.chapters),
        "word_count": sum(c.word_count for c in item.chapters),
        "created_at": item.created_at.isoformat() if item.created_at else None,
        "updated_at": item.updated_at.isoformat() if item.updated_at else None,
    }

def chapter_dict(item):
    return {
        "id": item.id,
        "novel_id": item.novel_id,
        "chapter_number": item.chapter_number,
        "title": item.title,
        "content": item.content,
        "word_count": item.word_count,
    }

def analysis_dict(item):
    return {
        "id": item.id,
        "novel_id": item.novel_id,
        "category": item.category,
        "name": item.name,
        "description": item.description,
        "data": item.data or {},
        "confirmed": item.confirmed,
    }

def split_chapters(text):
    pattern = re.compile(
        r"(?m)^\s*(第[零〇一二三四五六七八九十百千万两\d]+[章节回卷集].*)$"
        r"|^\s*((?:Chapter|CHAPTER)\s+\d+.*)$"
    )
    matches = list(pattern.finditer(text))
    if not matches:
        content = text.strip()
        return [("正文", content)] if content else []

    result = []
    if text[:matches[0].start()].strip():
        result.append(("序章", text[:matches[0].start()].strip()))

    for index, match in enumerate(matches):
        title = (match.group(1) or match.group(2) or "").strip()
        start = match.end()
        end = matches[index + 1].start() if index + 1 < len(matches) else len(text)
        content = text[start:end].strip()
        result.append((title or f"第{index + 1}章", content))

    return result

        raise HTTPException(404, "小说不存在")

    db.commit()
    db.refresh(item)
    return novel_dict(item)

@router.post("/novels/{novel_id}/import-txt")
async def import_txt(
    novel_id: int,
    file: UploadFile = File(...),
    db: Session = Depends(get_db),
):
    item = db.query(Novel).filter(Novel.id == novel_id).first()
    if not item:
        raise HTTPException(404, "小说不存在")

    filename = file.filename or "novel.txt"
    if Path(filename).suffix.lower() != ".txt":
        raise HTTPException(400, "目前仅支持 TXT 文件")

    raw = await file.read()
    if len(raw) > 100 * 1024 * 1024:
        raise HTTPException(413, "TXT 文件不能超过 100 MB")

    text = None
    for encoding in ("utf-8-sig", "utf-8", "gb18030"):
        try:
            text = raw.decode(encoding)
            break
        except UnicodeDecodeError:
            continue

    if text is None:
        raise HTTPException(400, "文件编码无法识别，请转换为 UTF-8")

    parts = split_chapters(text)
    if not parts:
        raise HTTPException(400, "文件内容为空")

    item.source_text = text
    item.title = item.title or Path(filename).stem

    for old in list(item.chapters):
        db.delete(old)

    for number, (title, content) in enumerate(parts, start=1):
        db.add(Chapter(
            novel_id=item.id,
            chapter_number=number,
            title=title[:300],
            content=content,
            word_count=len(re.sub(r"\s+", "", content)),
        ))

    db.commit()
    db.refresh(item)
    return {
        "success": True,
        "filename": filename,
        "chapter_count": len(parts),
        "word_count": len(re.sub(r"\s+", "", text)),
        "chapters": [chapter_dict(x) for x in item.chapters],
    }

def update_chapter(
    chapter_id: int,
    payload: ChapterUpdate,
    db: Session = Depends(get_db),
):
    item = db.query(Chapter).filter(Chapter.id == chapter_id).first()
    if not item:
        raise HTTPException(404, "章节不存在")
    values = payload.model_dump(exclude_unset=True)
    for key, value in values.items():
        if value is not None:
            setattr(item, key, value)
    item.word_count = len(re.sub(r"\s+", "", item.content))
    db.commit()
    db.refresh(item)
    return chapter_dict(item)

@router.post("/novels/{novel_id}/analyze")
def analyze_novel(novel_id: int, db: Session = Depends(get_db)):
    item = db.query(Novel).filter(Novel.id == novel_id).first()
    if not item:
        raise HTTPException(404, "小说不存在")
    if not item.chapters:
        raise HTTPException(400, "请先导入小说或创建章节")

    # 本地规则初步提取：这是基础识别，不冒充外部 AI 模型分析。
    full_text = "\n".join(c.content for c in item.chapters)
    candidates = []

    quoted_names = re.findall(r"[“「『]([^”」』]{2,12})[”」』]", full_text)
    for name in dict.fromkeys(quoted_names):
        candidates.append(("character", name, "从文本引号内容中提取的待核对名称"))

    scene_words = ("大厅", "宫殿", "山谷", "森林", "城池", "村庄", "洞穴", "学校", "医院")
    for word in scene_words:
        if word in full_text:
            candidates.append(("scene", word, "文本中出现的场景线索"))

    prop_words = ("宝剑", "长剑", "法宝", "玉佩", "戒指", "卷轴", "手机", "钥匙")
    for word in prop_words:
        if word in full_text:
            candidates.append(("prop", word, "文本中出现的道具线索"))

    event_words = ("突然", "于是", "最终", "随后", "大战", "相遇", "逃跑", "发现")
    for word in event_words:
        count = full_text.count(word)
        if count:
            candidates.append(("event", word, f"剧情事件线索，出现 {count} 次"))

    for old in db.query(AnalysisItem).filter(
        AnalysisItem.novel_id == novel_id,
        AnalysisItem.confirmed == False
    ).all():
        db.delete(old)

    existing = set()
    created = []
    for category, name, description in candidates:
        key = (category, name)
        if key in existing:
            continue
        existing.add(key)
        row = AnalysisItem(
            novel_id=novel_id,
            category=category,
            name=name,
            description=description,
            data={"source": "local_rule", "needs_review": True},
        )
        db.add(row)
        created.append(row)

    db.commit()
    return {
        "success": True,
        "mode": "local_rule_preliminary",
        "message": "基础文本线索提取完成。接入 AI 模型后可进行深度语义分析。",
        "items": [analysis_dict(x) for x in created],
        "total": len(created),
    }

@router.get("/novels/{novel_id}/analysis")

========== 文件：apps\api\app\routers\story.py ==========
from datetime import datetime
from typing import Optional

from fastapi import APIRouter, Depends, HTTPException
from pydantic import BaseModel, Field
from sqlalchemy.orm import Session
from sqlalchemy.inspection import inspect as sa_inspect

from app.database import get_db
from app.models.novel import Novel, Chapter
from app.models.story import StoryProject, EpisodePlan, StoryboardShot, StoryAssetLink
from app.models.visual import VisualAsset

router = APIRouter(prefix="/api/story", tags=["剧本改编与智能分镜"])


def serialize_model(obj):
    result = {}
    for column in sa_inspect(obj.__class__).mapper.column_attrs:
        value = getattr(obj, column.key)
        if isinstance(value, datetime):
            value = value.isoformat()
        result[column.key] = value
    return result


def chapter_value(chapter, candidates, default=""):
    for name in candidates:
        if hasattr(chapter, name):
            value = getattr(chapter, name)
            if value is not None and value != "":
                return value
    return default


def get_chapter_content(chapter):
    return str(chapter_value(
        chapter,
        ["content", "body", "text", "chapter_content", "original_text"],
        ""
    ))


def get_chapter_title(chapter):
    return str(chapter_value(
        chapter,
        ["title", "name", "chapter_title"],
        f"第{chapter.id}章"
    ))


def get_novel_chapters(db, novel_id):
    chapters = db.query(Chapter).all()
    result = []
    for chapter in chapters:
        chapter_novel_id = chapter_value(
            chapter, ["novel_id", "book_id"], None
        )
        if chapter_novel_id is not None and int(chapter_novel_id) == int(novel_id):
            result.append(chapter)

    result.sort(key=lambda x: chapter_value(
        x, ["chapter_number", "chapter_index", "order", "sort_order", "id"], x.id
    ))
    return result

    episode_ids: list[int]
    title: str = ""


class ShotInput(BaseModel):
    shot_number: int = 1
    scene_name: str = ""
    shot_type: str = "中景"
    camera_movement: str = "固定镜头"
    duration_seconds: float = 5
    visual_description: str = ""
    character_actions: str = ""
    dialogue: str = ""
    sound_effects: str = ""
    image_prompt: str = ""
    character_ids: list[int] = Field(default_factory=list)
    scene_asset_id: Optional[int] = None
    prop_ids: list[int] = Field(default_factory=list)
    status: str = "draft"
    sort_order: int = 0


class AssetLinkInput(BaseModel):
    asset_id: int
    role: str = "character"

    project = StoryProject(**payload.model_dump())
    db.add(project)
    db.commit()

    return {"success": True, "project": serialize_model(project)}

@router.get("/projects/{project_id}")

    project = db.query(StoryProject).filter(StoryProject.id == project_id).first()
    if not project:
        raise HTTPException(404, "剧本项目不存在")
    episodes = db.query(EpisodePlan).filter(
        EpisodePlan.project_id == project_id
    ).order_by(EpisodePlan.sort_order, EpisodePlan.episode_number).all()
    return {
        "project": serialize_model(project),
        "episodes": [serialize_model(x) for x in episodes],
    }


@router.post("/projects/{project_id}/plan")
def plan_episodes(project_id: int, db: Session = Depends(get_db)):
    project = db.query(StoryProject).filter(StoryProject.id == project_id).first()
    if not project:
        raise HTTPException(404, "剧本项目不存在")

    chapters = get_novel_chapters(db, project.novel_id)
    if not chapters:
        raise HTTPException(400, "该小说没有找到章节内容，无法规划分集")

    # 保留原章节顺序；超长章节允许拆成两集，不自动把短章节拆碎。
    old = db.query(EpisodePlan).filter(EpisodePlan.project_id == project_id).all()
    for item in old:
        db.delete(item)
    db.flush()

    episode_number = 1
    for chapter in chapters:
        content = get_chapter_content(chapter)
        title = get_chapter_title(chapter)
        if not content.strip():
            continue

        # 用章节内容长度估算分集；此处是初步规划规则，不冒充 AI 剧情分析。
        split = len(content) > 9000
        parts = [content[:len(content)//2], content[len(content)//2:]] if split else [content]

        for part_index, part in enumerate(parts):
            suffix = f"（下）" if split and part_index == 1 else (f"（上）" if split else "")
            episode = EpisodePlan(
                project_id=project_id,
                episode_number=episode_number,
                title=f"{title}{suffix}",
                source_chapter_ids=[chapter.id],
                source_text=part,
                synopsis="待 AI 根据原著内容提炼剧情摘要",
                screenplay="待 AI 改编为影视剧本",
                duration_seconds=project.target_duration,
                status="planned",
                sort_order=episode_number,
            )
            db.add(episode)
            episode_number += 1

    project.status = "planned"
    project.updated_at = datetime.utcnow()
    db.commit()

    episodes = db.query(EpisodePlan).filter(
        EpisodePlan.project_id == project_id
    ).order_by(EpisodePlan.sort_order).all()

    return {
        "success": True,
        "message": "初步分集计划已建立。剧情摘要和剧本正文仍需 AI 改编。",
        "episode_count": len(episodes),
        "episodes": [serialize_model(x) for x in episodes],
    }


@router.put("/episodes/{episode_id}")
def update_episode(episode_id: int, payload: EpisodeUpdate, db: Session = Depends(get_db)):
    episode = db.query(EpisodePlan).filter(EpisodePlan.id == episode_id).first()
    if not episode:
        raise HTTPException(404, "分集不存在")
    for key, value in payload.model_dump(exclude_unset=True).items():
        if key == "duration_seconds" and (value < 1 or value > 3600):
            raise HTTPException(400, "单集时长必须在 1 到 3600 秒之间")
        setattr(episode, key, value)
    episode.updated_at = datetime.utcnow()
    db.commit()
    return {"success": True, "episode": serialize_model(episode)}


@router.delete("/episodes/{episode_id}")
def delete_episode(episode_id: int, db: Session = Depends(get_db)):
    episode = db.query(EpisodePlan).filter(EpisodePlan.id == episode_id).first()
    if not episode:
        raise HTTPException(404, "分集不存在")
    db.query(StoryboardShot).filter(StoryboardShot.episode_id == episode_id).delete()
    db.query(StoryAssetLink).filter(StoryAssetLink.episode_id == episode_id).delete()
    db.delete(episode)
    db.commit()
    return {"success": True}


@router.post("/projects/{project_id}/reorder")
def reorder_episodes(project_id: int, payload: EpisodeReorder, db: Session = Depends(get_db)):
    episodes = db.query(EpisodePlan).filter(
        EpisodePlan.project_id == project_id
    ).all()
    lookup = {x.id: x for x in episodes}
    if set(payload.episode_ids) != set(lookup.keys()):
        raise HTTPException(400, "排序列表必须包含该项目全部分集，且不能重复")
    for index, episode_id in enumerate(payload.episode_ids, start=1):
        lookup[episode_id].sort_order = index
        lookup[episode_id].episode_number = index
    db.commit()
    return {"success": True}


@router.post("/projects/{project_id}/merge")
def merge_episodes(project_id: int, payload: EpisodeMerge, db: Session = Depends(get_db)):
    if len(payload.episode_ids) < 2:
        raise HTTPException(400, "至少选择两集才能合并")
    episodes = db.query(EpisodePlan).filter(
        EpisodePlan.project_id == project_id,
        EpisodePlan.id.in_(payload.episode_ids)
    ).order_by(EpisodePlan.sort_order).all()
    if len(episodes) != len(set(payload.episode_ids)):
        raise HTTPException(400, "分集不存在或不属于该项目")

    first = episodes[0]
    first.title = payload.title.strip() or " + ".join(x.title for x in episodes)
    first.source_text = "\n\n".join(x.source_text or "" for x in episodes)
    first.source_chapter_ids = list(dict.fromkeys(
        chapter_id for x in episodes for chapter_id in (x.source_chapter_ids or [])
    ))
    first.duration_seconds = min(
        3600, sum(x.duration_seconds for x in episodes)
    )
    for item in episodes[1:]:
        db.query(StoryboardShot).filter(StoryboardShot.episode_id == item.id).update(
            {StoryboardShot.episode_id: first.id}
        )
        db.query(StoryAssetLink).filter(StoryAssetLink.episode_id == item.id).update(
            {StoryAssetLink.episode_id: first.id}
        )
        db.delete(item)
    db.commit()
    return {"success": True, "merged_episode_id": first.id}


@router.post("/episodes/{episode_id}/split")
def split_episode(episode_id: int, db: Session = Depends(get_db)):
    episode = db.query(EpisodePlan).filter(EpisodePlan.id == episode_id).first()
    if not episode:
        raise HTTPException(404, "分集不存在")
    text = episode.source_text or ""
    if len(text.strip()) < 2:
        raise HTTPException(400, "原始内容太短，无法拆分")
    midpoint = len(text) // 2
    split_at = text.find("\n", midpoint - 500, midpoint + 500)
    if split_at < 0:
        split_at = midpoint

    second = EpisodePlan(
        project_id=episode.project_id,
        episode_number=episode.episode_number + 1,
        title=episode.title + "（下）",
        source_chapter_ids=episode.source_chapter_ids or [],
        source_text=text[split_at:],
        synopsis="待 AI 提炼剧情摘要",
        screenplay="待 AI 改编剧本",
        duration_seconds=episode.duration_seconds,
        status="planned",
        sort_order=episode.sort_order + 1,
    )
    episode.title = episode.title + "（上）"
    episode.source_text = text[:split_at]
    db.add(second)
    db.flush()

    later = db.query(EpisodePlan).filter(
        EpisodePlan.project_id == episode.project_id,
        EpisodePlan.id != episode.id,
        EpisodePlan.sort_order >= second.sort_order
    ).all()
    for item in later:
        item.sort_order += 1
        item.episode_number += 1

    db.commit()
    return {"success": True, "new_episode": serialize_model(second)}

@router.post("/shots/{shot_id}/assets")
def link_asset(shot_id: int, payload: AssetLinkInput, db: Session = Depends(get_db)):
    shot = db.query(StoryboardShot).filter(StoryboardShot.id == shot_id).first()
    asset = db.query(VisualAsset).filter(VisualAsset.id == payload.asset_id).first()
    if not shot or not asset:
        raise HTTPException(404, "分镜或视觉素材不存在")
    link = StoryAssetLink(
        project_id=shot.project_id,
        episode_id=shot.episode_id,
        shot_id=shot.id,
        asset_id=asset.id,
        role=payload.role,
    )
    db.add(link)
    db.commit()
    return {"success": True, "link_id": link.id}


@router.get("/shots/{shot_id}/assets")
def get_shot_assets(shot_id: int, db: Session = Depends(get_db)):
    links = db.query(StoryAssetLink).filter(StoryAssetLink.shot_id == shot_id).all()
    result = []
    for link in links:
        asset = db.query(VisualAsset).filter(VisualAsset.id == link.asset_id).first()
        if asset:
            result.append({"role": link.role, "asset": serialize_model(asset)})
    return result


========== 文件：apps\api\app\routers\studio.py ==========
import os
import uuid
import json
import urllib.request
import urllib.error
from pathlib import Path
from datetime import datetime
from fastapi import APIRouter, Depends, HTTPException, UploadFile, File, Form
from pydantic import BaseModel
from sqlalchemy.orm import Session
from cryptography.fernet import Fernet, InvalidToken
from app.database import get_db, ROOT_DIR
from app.models.studio import (
    StudioResource, AIProviderConfig, AIRuntimeSetting,
    AIUsageLog, MediaAsset
)

router = APIRouter(prefix="/api", tags=["AI管理与素材中心"])

SECRET_DIR = ROOT_DIR / "storage" / "secrets"
SECRET_DIR.mkdir(parents=True, exist_ok=True)
KEY_FILE = SECRET_DIR / "local.key"
ASSET_DIR = ROOT_DIR / "storage" / "assets"
ASSET_DIR.mkdir(parents=True, exist_ok=True)

def get_cipher():
    if not KEY_FILE.exists():
        KEY_FILE.write_bytes(Fernet.generate_key())
    return Fernet(KEY_FILE.read_bytes())

def encrypt_key(value):
    if not value:
        return ""
    return get_cipher().encrypt(value.encode("utf-8")).decode("utf-8")

def decrypt_key(value):
    if not value:
        return ""
    try:
        return get_cipher().decrypt(value.encode("utf-8")).decode("utf-8")
    except InvalidToken:
        raise HTTPException(500, "API密钥解密失败，请检查本地密钥文件")

def runtime(db):
    row = db.query(AIRuntimeSetting).first()
    if not row:
        row = AIRuntimeSetting(network_enabled=False, paid_api_allowed=False)
        db.add(row)
        db.commit()
        db.refresh(row)
    return row

def provider_dict(x):
    return {
        "id": x.id,
        "provider": x.provider,
        "display_name": x.display_name,
        "base_url": x.base_url,
        "model": x.model,
        "enabled": x.enabled,
        "paid": x.paid,
        "purpose": x.purpose,
        "has_api_key": bool(x.encrypted_api_key),
    }

def resource_dict(x):
    return {
        "id": x.id,
        "category": x.category,
        "name": x.name,
        "description": x.description,
        "data": x.data or {},
        "image_path": x.image_path,
        "confirmed": x.confirmed,
        "created_at": x.created_at.isoformat() if x.created_at else None,
    }

def asset_dict(x):
    return {
        "id": x.id,
        "filename": x.filename,
        "category": x.category,
        "mime_type": x.mime_type,
        "file_size": x.file_size,
        "description": x.description,
        "url": f"/api/assets/{x.id}/download",
        "created_at": x.created_at.isoformat() if x.created_at else None,
    }

class ProviderInput(BaseModel):
    provider: str
    display_name: str
    base_url: str
    model: str
    api_key: str = ""
    enabled: bool = False
    paid: bool = True
    purpose: str = "text"

class RuntimeInput(BaseModel):
    network_enabled: bool
    paid_api_allowed: bool

class ResourceInput(BaseModel):
    name: str
    description: str = ""
    data: dict = {}
    image_path: str = ""
    confirmed: bool = False

class ResourceUpdate(BaseModel):
    name: str | None = None
    description: str | None = None
    data: dict | None = None
    image_path: str | None = None
    confirmed: bool | None = None

class ChatInput(BaseModel):
    provider: str
    messages: list[dict]
    confirm_paid: bool = False
    task: str = "chat"

@router.get("/ai/providers")
def list_providers(db: Session = Depends(get_db)):
    items = db.query(AIProviderConfig).order_by(AIProviderConfig.id).all()
    return {"items": [provider_dict(x) for x in items]}

@router.post("/ai/providers")
def save_provider(payload: ProviderInput, db: Session = Depends(get_db)):
    provider_id = payload.provider.strip().lower()
    if not provider_id:
        raise HTTPException(400, "服务商标识不能为空")

    item = db.query(AIProviderConfig).filter(
        AIProviderConfig.provider == provider_id
    ).first()

    if not item:
        item = AIProviderConfig(provider=provider_id)
        db.add(item)

    item.display_name = payload.display_name
    item.base_url = payload.base_url.rstrip("/")
    item.model = payload.model
    item.enabled = payload.enabled
    item.paid = payload.paid
    item.purpose = payload.purpose

    if payload.api_key.strip():
        item.encrypted_api_key = encrypt_key(payload.api_key.strip())

    db.commit()
    db.refresh(item)
    return provider_dict(item)

@router.delete("/ai/providers/{provider}")
def delete_provider(provider: str, db: Session = Depends(get_db)):
    item = db.query(AIProviderConfig).filter(
        AIProviderConfig.provider == provider
    ).first()
    if not item:
        raise HTTPException(404, "模型配置不存在")
    db.delete(item)
    db.commit()
    return {"success": True}

@router.post("/ai/chat")
def ai_chat(payload: ChatInput, db: Session = Depends(get_db)):
    settings = runtime(db)
    config = db.query(AIProviderConfig).filter(
        AIProviderConfig.provider == payload.provider.lower()
    ).first()

    if not settings.network_enabled:
        raise HTTPException(403, "网络调用总开关已关闭")
    if not config or not config.enabled:
        raise HTTPException(400, "模型不存在或尚未启用")
    if not config.base_url or not config.model or not config.encrypted_api_key:
        raise HTTPException(400, "请先完整配置模型地址、模型名称和 API 密钥")
    if config.paid and not (settings.paid_api_allowed and payload.confirm_paid):
        raise HTTPException(403, "该模型标记为付费服务，必须开启付费授权并在本次请求明确确认")

    api_key = decrypt_key(config.encrypted_api_key)
    url = config.base_url.rstrip("/") + "/chat/completions"
    body = json.dumps({
        "model": config.model,
        "messages": payload.messages,
    }).encode("utf-8")

    request = urllib.request.Request(
        url,
        data=body,
        headers={
            "Content-Type": "application/json",
            "Authorization": f"Bearer {api_key}",
        },
        method="POST",
    )

    try:
        with urllib.request.urlopen(request, timeout=120) as response:
            result = json.loads(response.read().decode("utf-8"))
        db.add(AIUsageLog(
            provider=config.provider,
            model=config.model,
            task=payload.task,
            status="success",
            detail="请求成功",
        ))
        db.commit()
        return {
            "success": True,
            "provider": config.provider,
            "model": config.model,
            "result": result,
        }
    except urllib.error.HTTPError as exc:
        detail = exc.read().decode("utf-8", errors="replace")[:3000]
        db.add(AIUsageLog(
            provider=config.provider,
            model=config.model,
            task=payload.task,
            status="failed",
            detail=detail,
        ))
        db.commit()
        raise HTTPException(exc.code, f"模型服务返回错误：{detail}")
    except Exception as exc:
        db.add(AIUsageLog(
            provider=config.provider,
            model=config.model,
            task=payload.task,
            status="failed",
            detail=str(exc)[:2000],
        ))
        db.commit()
        raise HTTPException(502, f"模型请求失败：{str(exc)}")

RESOURCE_CATEGORIES = {
    "characters", "relationships", "scenes", "props", "world",
    "events", "scripts", "storyboards"
}

@router.get("/studio/{category}")

        raise HTTPException(404, "不支持的素材分类")
    items = db.query(StudioResource).filter(
        StudioResource.category == category
    ).order_by(StudioResource.updated_at.desc()).all()
    return {"items": [resource_dict(x) for x in items], "total": len(items)}

    category: str,

    db: Session = Depends(get_db),
):

        raise HTTPException(404, "不支持的素材分类")

        description=payload.description,
        data=payload.data,
        image_path=payload.image_path,
        confirmed=payload.confirmed,
    )
    db.add(item)
    db.commit()
    db.refresh(item)
    return resource_dict(item)

    item_id: int,
    payload: ResourceUpdate,
    db: Session = Depends(get_db),
):
    item = db.query(StudioResource).filter(
        StudioResource.id == item_id
    ).first()
    if not item:
        raise HTTPException(404, "素材记录不存在")
    for key, value in payload.model_dump(exclude_unset=True).items():
        if value is not None:
            setattr(item, key, value)
    db.commit()
    db.refresh(item)
    return resource_dict(item)

@router.delete("/studio/items/{item_id}")

        StudioResource.id == item_id
    ).first()
    if not item:
        raise HTTPException(404, "素材记录不存在")
    db.delete(item)
    db.commit()
    return {"success": True}

@router.post("/assets/upload")
async def upload_asset(
    file: UploadFile = File(...),
    category: str = Form("other"),
    description: str = Form(""),
    db: Session = Depends(get_db),
):
    allowed = {"image", "audio", "video", "other"}
    if category not in allowed:
        raise HTTPException(400, "素材分类无效")

    original = Path(file.filename or "upload.bin").name
    suffix = Path(original).suffix.lower()
    stored = f"{uuid.uuid4().hex}{suffix}"
    destination = ASSET_DIR / stored

    size = 0
    try:
        with destination.open("wb") as output:
            while True:
                chunk = await file.read(1024 * 1024)
                if not chunk:
                    break
                size += len(chunk)
                if size > 2 * 1024 * 1024 * 1024:
                    raise HTTPException(413, "单个素材文件不能超过 2 GB")
                output.write(chunk)

        item = MediaAsset(
            filename=original,
            stored_name=stored,
            category=category,
            mime_type=file.content_type or "",
            file_size=size,
            file_path=str(destination),
            description=description,
        )
        db.add(item)
        db.commit()
        db.refresh(item)
        return asset_dict(item)
    except Exception:
        if destination.exists():
            destination.unlink()
        raise

@router.get("/assets")
def list_assets(category: str | None = None, db: Session = Depends(get_db)):
    query = db.query(MediaAsset)
    if category:
        query = query.filter(MediaAsset.category == category)
    items = query.order_by(MediaAsset.created_at.desc()).limit(1000).all()
    return {"items": [asset_dict(x) for x in items], "total": len(items)}

@router.get("/assets/{asset_id}/download")
def download_asset(asset_id: int, db: Session = Depends(get_db)):
    from fastapi.responses import FileResponse
    item = db.query(MediaAsset).filter(MediaAsset.id == asset_id).first()
    if not item or not Path(item.file_path).exists():
        raise HTTPException(404, "文件不存在")
    return FileResponse(
        item.file_path,
        media_type=item.mime_type or "application/octet-stream",
        filename=item.filename,
    )

@router.delete("/assets/{asset_id}")
def delete_asset(asset_id: int, db: Session = Depends(get_db)):
    item = db.query(MediaAsset).filter(MediaAsset.id == asset_id).first()
    if not item:
        raise HTTPException(404, "素材不存在")
    path = Path(item.file_path)
    if path.exists():
        path.unlink()
    db.delete(item)
    db.commit()
    return {"success": True}


========== 文件：apps\api\app\routers\video_editor.py ==========
import os
import uuid
from pathlib import Path
from typing import Optional

from fastapi import APIRouter, Depends, HTTPException
from pydantic import BaseModel, Field
from sqlalchemy.orm import Session

from app.database import get_db
from app.models.editing import VideoEditJob
from app.models.media import MediaFile
from app.services.editing_service import (
    call_ai_for_edit_plan, validate_plan, execute_edit_plan
)
from app.services.media_service import safe_storage_file

router = APIRouter(prefix="/api/video-editor", tags=["AI 视频剪辑"])


class CreateEditInput(BaseModel):
    title: str = "视频剪辑任务"
    provider: str
    model: str
    instruction: str = Field(min_length=2, max_length=12000)
    source_paths: list[str] = Field(min_length=1, max_length=30)
    confirm_paid: bool = False


class ExecuteEditInput(BaseModel):
    subtitle_path: Optional[str] = None
    audio_path: Optional[str] = None


@router.post("/jobs")
def create_edit_job(data: CreateEditInput, db: Session = Depends(get_db)):
    try:
        sources = []
        for raw in data.source_paths:
            path = safe_storage_file(raw)
            sources.append(str(path))

        job = VideoEditJob(
            title=data.title,
            provider=data.provider,
            model=data.model,
            instruction=data.instruction,
            status="analyzing",
            source_files=sources
        )
        db.add(job)
        db.commit()
        db.refresh(job)

        plan, raw_response = call_ai_for_edit_plan(
            db, data.provider, data.model,
            data.instruction, sources, data.confirm_paid
        )
        validated = validate_plan(plan, sources)

        job.edit_plan = validated
        job.title = validated["title"] or data.title
        job.status = "planned"
        db.commit()

        return {
            "id": job.id,
            "status": job.status,
            "plan": validated
        }
    except Exception as exc:
        db.rollback()
        raise HTTPException(502, f"AI 剪辑方案生成失败：{exc}")


@router.get("/jobs")
def list_edit_jobs(db: Session = Depends(get_db)):
    rows = db.query(VideoEditJob).order_by(
        VideoEditJob.id.desc()
    ).limit(200).all()

    return [{
        "id": x.id,
        "title": x.title,
        "provider": x.provider,
        "model": x.model,
        "instruction": x.instruction,
        "status": x.status,
        "edit_plan": x.edit_plan,
        "output_path": x.output_path,
        "error": x.error,
        "created_at": x.created_at
    } for x in rows]


@router.get("/jobs/{job_id}")
def get_edit_job(job_id: int, db: Session = Depends(get_db)):
    job = db.query(VideoEditJob).filter(
        VideoEditJob.id == job_id
    ).first()
    if not job:
        raise HTTPException(404, "剪辑任务不存在")

    return {
        "id": job.id,
        "title": job.title,
        "status": job.status,
        "edit_plan": job.edit_plan,
        "output_path": job.output_path,
        "error": job.error
    }


@router.post("/jobs/{job_id}/execute")
def execute_job(job_id: int, data: ExecuteEditInput,
                db: Session = Depends(get_db)):
    job = db.query(VideoEditJob).filter(
        VideoEditJob.id == job_id
    ).first()
    if not job:
        raise HTTPException(404, "剪辑任务不存在")

    if job.status != "planned" or not job.edit_plan:
        raise HTTPException(400, "任务尚未生成有效剪辑方案")

    try:
        job.status = "rendering"
        db.commit()

        output, size = execute_edit_plan(
            job.edit_plan,
            subtitle_path=data.subtitle_path,
            audio_path=data.audio_path
        )

        job.output_path = output
        job.status = "completed"
        job.error = None

        media = MediaFile(
            name=Path(output).name,
            category="video",
            file_path=output,
            mime_type="video/mp4",
            file_size=size
        )
        db.add(media)
        db.commit()

        return {
            "id": job.id,
            "status": job.status,
            "output_path": output,
            "size": size
        }
    except Exception as exc:
        job.status = "failed"
        job.error = str(exc)
        db.commit()
        raise HTTPException(500, f"视频剪辑执行失败：{exc}")


@router.get("/health")
def editor_health():
    return {"video_editor": "ready", "engine": "FFmpeg"}

import os
import json
import base64
import mimetypes
import urllib.request
import urllib.error
from pathlib import Path
from datetime import datetime
from typing import Optional

from fastapi import APIRouter, Depends, HTTPException, UploadFile, File, Form
from pydantic import BaseModel
from sqlalchemy.orm import Session
from cryptography.fernet import Fernet, InvalidToken

from app.database import get_db, ROOT_DIR
from app.models.visual import VisualAsset, ImageTask
from app.models.studio import AIProviderConfig, AIRuntimeSetting

router = APIRouter(prefix="/api/visual", tags=["AI视觉素材"])

ROOT = ROOT_DIR
ASSET_DIR = ROOT / "storage" / "visual-assets"
REFERENCE_DIR = ROOT / "storage" / "reference-images"
ASSET_DIR.mkdir(parents=True, exist_ok=True)
REFERENCE_DIR.mkdir(parents=True, exist_ok=True)


class AssetInput(BaseModel):
    category: str
    name: str
    description: str = ""
    appearance: str = ""
    clothing: str = ""
    personality: str = ""
    consistency_prompt: str = ""
    style: str = "3D动漫电影风格"
    data: dict = {}


class GenerateInput(BaseModel):
    asset_id: Optional[int] = None
    provider: str = "doubao"
    task_type: str = "character"
    prompt: str
    size: str = "2K"
    confirm_paid: bool = False


def decrypt_key(encrypted):
    if not encrypted:
        return ""
    key_file = ROOT / "storage" / "secrets" / "local.key"
    if not key_file.exists():
        raise HTTPException(400, "本地加密密钥不存在，无法读取服务商 API Key")
    try:
        key = key_file.read_bytes()
        return Fernet(key).decrypt(encrypted.encode()).decode()
    except (InvalidToken, ValueError):
        raise HTTPException(400, "API Key 解密失败，请重新保存服务商配置")


def get_runtime(db):
    setting = db.query(AIRuntimeSetting).first()
    if not setting:
        return {"network_enabled": False, "paid_api_allowed": False}
    return {
        "network_enabled": bool(setting.network_enabled),
        "paid_api_allowed": bool(setting.paid_api_allowed),
    }


@router.get("/assets")
def list_assets(category: Optional[str] = None, db: Session = Depends(get_db)):

    if category:
        query = query.filter(VisualAsset.category == category)
    items = query.order_by(VisualAsset.created_at.desc()).all()
    return [{

        "category": x.category,
        "name": x.name,
        "description": x.description,
        "appearance": x.appearance,
        "clothing": x.clothing,
        "personality": x.personality,
        "consistency_prompt": x.consistency_prompt,
        "style": x.style,
        "image_path": x.image_path,
        "reference_path": x.reference_path,
        "confirmed": x.confirmed,
        "data": x.data or {},
        "created_at": x.created_at.isoformat() if x.created_at else None,
    } for x in items]


@router.post("/assets")
def create_asset(payload: AssetInput, db: Session = Depends(get_db)):
    allowed = {"characters", "scenes", "props"}
    if payload.category not in allowed:
        raise HTTPException(400, "素材分类只支持 characters、scenes、props")
    item = VisualAsset(**payload.model_dump())
    db.add(item)
    db.commit()
    db.refresh(item)
    return {"success": True, "id": item.id}


@router.put("/assets/{asset_id}")
def update_asset(asset_id: int, payload: AssetInput, db: Session = Depends(get_db)):
    item = db.query(VisualAsset).filter(VisualAsset.id == asset_id).first()
    if not item:
        raise HTTPException(404, "素材不存在")
    for key, value in payload.model_dump().items():
        setattr(item, key, value)
    item.updated_at = datetime.utcnow()
    db.commit()
    return {"success": True}


@router.delete("/assets/{asset_id}")

        raise HTTPException(404, "素材不存在")
    db.delete(item)
    db.commit()
    return {"success": True}


@router.post("/references")
async def upload_reference(
    file: UploadFile = File(...),
    asset_id: Optional[int] = Form(None),
    db: Session = Depends(get_db)
):
    suffix = Path(file.filename or "reference.png").suffix[:12] or ".png"
    filename = f"reference_{datetime.now().strftime('%Y%m%d%H%M%S%f')}{suffix}"
    destination = REFERENCE_DIR / filename
    content = await file.read()
    if len(content) > 100 * 1024 * 1024:
        raise HTTPException(413, "参考图片不能超过 100 MB")
    destination.write_bytes(content)

    if asset_id:
        item = db.query(VisualAsset).filter(VisualAsset.id == asset_id).first()
        if not item:
            raise HTTPException(404, "指定素材不存在")
        item.reference_path = str(destination)
        db.commit()

    return {"success": True, "filename": filename, "path": str(destination)}


@router.get("/references/{filename}")
def get_reference(filename: str):
    from fastapi.responses import FileResponse
    path = (REFERENCE_DIR / Path(filename).name).resolve()
    if not path.exists() or REFERENCE_DIR.resolve() not in path.parents:
        raise HTTPException(404, "参考图片不存在")
    return FileResponse(path)

@router.post("/tasks")
def create_task(payload: GenerateInput, db: Session = Depends(get_db)):
    if not payload.prompt.strip():
        raise HTTPException(400, "图片提示词不能为空")
    if payload.asset_id:
        asset = db.query(VisualAsset).filter(VisualAsset.id == payload.asset_id).first()
        if not asset:
            raise HTTPException(404, "关联素材不存在")

    task = ImageTask(
        asset_id=payload.asset_id,
        provider=payload.provider,
        task_type=payload.task_type,
        prompt=payload.prompt.strip(),
        size=payload.size,
        status="pending",
    )
    db.add(task)
    db.commit()
    db.refresh(task)
    return {"success": True, "task_id": task.id, "status": task.status}


@router.post("/tasks/{task_id}/run")
def run_task(task_id: int, confirm_paid: bool = False, db: Session = Depends(get_db)):
    task = db.query(ImageTask).filter(ImageTask.id == task_id).first()
    if not task:
        raise HTTPException(404, "图片任务不存在")
    if task.status == "completed":
        return {"success": True, "task_id": task.id, "status": task.status, "result_path": task.result_path}
    if task.status == "running":
        raise HTTPException(409, "任务正在执行")

    runtime = get_runtime(db)
    if not runtime["network_enabled"]:
        raise HTTPException(403, "网络调用总开关已关闭，请先到 AI 管理中心开启")
    
    provider = db.query(AIProviderConfig).filter(
        AIProviderConfig.provider == task.provider
    ).first()
    if not provider or not provider.enabled:
        raise HTTPException(400, f"{task.provider} 服务商尚未配置或未启用")
    if not provider.encrypted_api_key:
        raise HTTPException(400, "服务商尚未保存 API Key")
    if bool(provider.paid) and (not runtime["paid_api_allowed"] or not confirm_paid):
        raise HTTPException(403, "该服务商可能产生费用。请开启付费授权，并在本次任务中明确确认")

    task.status = "running"
    task.error = ""
    db.commit()

    try:
        api_key = decrypt_key(provider.encrypted_api_key)
        base_url = (provider.base_url or "").rstrip("/")
        if not base_url:
            raise ValueError("服务商 Base URL 未配置")

        # 豆包 Ark 图片生成接口采用 OpenAI 风格的 images/generations 请求格式。
        endpoint = base_url if base_url.endswith("/images/generations") else base_url + "/images/generations"
        body = {
            "model": provider.model,
            "prompt": task.prompt,
            "size": task.size,
            "response_format": "url",
        }
        request = urllib.request.Request(
            endpoint,
            data=json.dumps(body, ensure_ascii=False).encode("utf-8"),
            headers={
                "Authorization": f"Bearer {api_key}",
                "Content-Type": "application/json",
            },
            method="POST",
        )
        with urllib.request.urlopen(request, timeout=300) as response:
            result = json.loads(response.read().decode("utf-8"))

        data = result.get("data") or []
        if not data:
            raise ValueError("图片服务没有返回图片数据：" + json.dumps(result, ensure_ascii=False)[:1000])

        first = data[0]
        image_url = first.get("url", "")
        b64_data = first.get("b64_json", "")

        if b64_data:
            image_bytes = base64.b64decode(b64_data)
            filename = f"image_task_{task.id}.png"
            output = ASSET_DIR / filename
            output.write_bytes(image_bytes)
            task.result_path = str(output)
        elif image_url:
            task.result_url = image_url
            download_request = urllib.request.Request(image_url)
            with urllib.request.urlopen(download_request, timeout=300) as response:
                image_bytes = response.read()
            filename = f"image_task_{task.id}.png"
            output = ASSET_DIR / filename
            output.write_bytes(image_bytes)
            task.result_path = str(output)
        else:
            raise ValueError("服务商返回结果中没有图片 URL 或 b64_json")

        task.model = provider.model
        task.status = "completed"
        task.finished_at = datetime.utcnow()

        if task.asset_id:
            asset = db.query(VisualAsset).filter(VisualAsset.id == task.asset_id).first()
            if asset:
                asset.image_path = task.result_path
                asset.updated_at = datetime.utcnow()

        db.commit()
        return {
            "success": True,
            "task_id": task.id,
            "status": task.status,
            "result_path": task.result_path,
            "result_url": task.result_url,
        }

    except urllib.error.HTTPError as exc:
        detail = exc.read().decode("utf-8", errors="replace")[:2000]
        task.status = "failed"
        task.error = f"图片服务 HTTP {exc.code}: {detail}"
        db.commit()
        raise HTTPException(502, task.error)
    except Exception as exc:
        task.status = "failed"
        task.error = str(exc)[:2000]
        db.commit()
        raise HTTPException(500, task.error)


@router.get("/files/{filename}")

    path = (ASSET_DIR / Path(filename).name).resolve()
    if not path.exists() or ASSET_DIR.resolve() not in path.parents:
        raise HTTPException(404, "图片文件不存在")
    return FileResponse(path)

========== 文件：apps\api\app\services\editing_service.py ==========
import json
import re
import uuid
import subprocess
import urllib.request
import urllib.error
from pathlib import Path

from app.services.media_service import (
    STORAGE, VIDEO_DIR, safe_storage_file, run_ffmpeg
)

EDIT_DIR = STORAGE / "edit-jobs"
EDIT_DIR.mkdir(parents=True, exist_ok=True)


def decrypt_key(config):
    from app.services.media_service import decrypt_api_key

    encrypted = (
        getattr(config, "api_key_encrypted", None)
        or getattr(config, "encrypted_api_key", None)
    )
    if not encrypted:
        raise RuntimeError("AI 服务商没有保存 API Key")
    return decrypt_api_key(encrypted)


def get_ai_config(db, provider_name):
    from app.models.studio import AIProviderConfig, AIRuntimeSetting

    config = db.query(AIProviderConfig).filter(
        AIProviderConfig.provider == provider_name
    ).first()

    if not config:
        raise RuntimeError(f"AI 服务商 {provider_name} 尚未配置")

    if not getattr(config, "enabled", False):
        raise RuntimeError("该 AI 服务商尚未启用")

    runtime = db.query(AIRuntimeSetting).first()
    if not runtime or not getattr(runtime, "network_enabled", False):
        raise RuntimeError("全局网络调用开关已关闭")

    paid = bool(getattr(config, "paid", True))
    if paid and not getattr(runtime, "paid_api_allowed", False):
        raise RuntimeError("付费 API 总授权未开启")

    base_url = getattr(config, "base_url", None)
    if not base_url:
        raise RuntimeError("AI 服务商缺少 Base URL")

    return config, decrypt_key(config), base_url.rstrip("/")


def call_ai_for_edit_plan(db, provider, model, instruction, source_files,
                          confirm_paid=False):
    config, key, base = get_ai_config(db, provider)

    if bool(getattr(config, "paid", True)) and not confirm_paid:
        raise RuntimeError("本次 AI 剪辑分析可能产生费用，请先明确确认")

    system_prompt = """
你是专业影视剪辑师。你必须把用户的剪辑要求转换成严格 JSON。
只能使用用户提供的视频文件路径，不得编造路径。
只返回 JSON，不要 Markdown，不要解释。

JSON 格式：
{
  "title": "剪辑项目名称",
  "summary": "剪辑方案说明",
  "segments": [
    {
      "file_path": "必须是输入文件中的路径",
      "start": 0,
      "end": 10,
      "description": "该片段的用途"
    }
  ],
  "width": 1920,
  "height": 1080,
  "subtitle_text": "",
  "notes": []
}

规则：
1. start 和 end 是视频原始时间，单位秒。
2. end 必须大于 start。
3. 片段必须来自用户提供的文件。
4. 按用户要求排序 segments。
5. 不要编造画面内容；无法判断的内容写入 notes。
6. 用户没有要求删除内容时，尽量保留相关内容。
7. 不要在 JSON 外输出任何文字。
"""

    user_prompt = (
        "用户剪辑要求：\n" + instruction +
        "\n\n允许使用的视频文件：\n" +
        json.dumps(source_files, ensure_ascii=False)
    )

    payload = {
        "model": model,
        "temperature": 0.2,
        "messages": [
            {"role": "system", "content": system_prompt},
            {"role": "user", "content": user_prompt}
        ]
    }

    request = urllib.request.Request(
        base + "/chat/completions",
        data=json.dumps(payload, ensure_ascii=False).encode("utf-8"),
        headers={
            "Authorization": f"Bearer {key}",
            "Content-Type": "application/json"
        },
        method="POST"
    )

    try:
        with urllib.request.urlopen(request, timeout=300) as response:
            result = json.loads(response.read().decode("utf-8"))
    except urllib.error.HTTPError as exc:
        detail = exc.read().decode("utf-8", errors="replace")
        raise RuntimeError(f"AI 剪辑分析 API 错误 HTTP {exc.code}: {detail[:2000]}")
    except Exception as exc:
        raise RuntimeError(f"AI 剪辑分析请求失败：{exc}")

    content = (
        result.get("choices", [{}])[0]
        .get("message", {})
        .get("content", "")
    )

    if isinstance(content, list):
        content = "".join(
            part.get("text", "") for part in content
            if isinstance(part, dict)
        )

    content = str(content).strip()
    content = re.sub(r"^```(?:json)?\s*", "", content)
    content = re.sub(r"\s*```$", "", content)

    try:
        plan = json.loads(content)
    except Exception as exc:
        raise RuntimeError("AI 返回的剪辑方案不是有效 JSON：" + content[:1500]) from exc

    if not isinstance(plan, dict) or not isinstance(plan.get("segments"), list):
        raise RuntimeError("AI 返回的剪辑方案缺少 segments")

    return plan, result


def validate_plan(plan, allowed_files):
    if not plan.get("segments"):
        raise ValueError("剪辑方案没有可执行的视频片段")

    allowed = {str(Path(x).resolve()) for x in allowed_files}
    validated = []

    for index, segment in enumerate(plan["segments"], 1):
        if not isinstance(segment, dict):
            raise ValueError(f"第 {index} 个片段格式错误")

        raw_path = segment.get("file_path")
        if not raw_path:
            raise ValueError(f"第 {index} 个片段缺少视频路径")

        path = safe_storage_file(raw_path)
        if str(path.resolve()) not in allowed:
            raise ValueError(f"第 {index} 个片段使用了未授权的输入文件")

        start = float(segment.get("start", 0))
        end = float(segment.get("end", 0))

        if start < 0 or end <= start:
            raise ValueError(f"第 {index} 个片段时间范围无效")

        if end - start > 7200:
            raise ValueError(f"第 {index} 个片段时长超过限制")

        validated.append({
            "file_path": str(path),
            "start": start,
            "end": end,
            "description": str(segment.get("description", ""))[:1000]
        })

    width = int(plan.get("width", 1920))
    height = int(plan.get("height", 1080))

    if not (256 <= width <= 3840 and 256 <= height <= 3840):
        raise ValueError("输出分辨率超出允许范围")

    return {
        "title": str(plan.get("title", "视频剪辑"))[:300],
        "summary": str(plan.get("summary", ""))[:5000],
        "segments": validated,
        "width": width,
        "height": height,
        "subtitle_text": str(plan.get("subtitle_text", ""))[:10000],
        "notes": plan.get("notes", [])
    }


def execute_edit_plan(plan, subtitle_path=None, audio_path=None):
    work = EDIT_DIR / uuid.uuid4().hex
    work.mkdir(parents=True, exist_ok=True)

    clips = []

    for index, segment in enumerate(plan["segments"]):
        output = work / f"segment_{index:04d}.mp4"
        duration = segment["end"] - segment["start"]

        args = [
            "-ss", str(segment["start"]),
            "-i", segment["file_path"],
            "-t", str(duration),
            "-vf",
            f"scale={plan['width']}:{plan['height']}:force_original_aspect_ratio=decrease,"
            f"pad={plan['width']}:{plan['height']}:(ow-iw)/2:(oh-ih)/2",
            "-c:v", "libx264",
            "-preset", "veryfast",
            "-crf", "21",
            "-c:a", "aac",
            "-ar", "48000",
            "-ac", "2",
            "-avoid_negative_ts", "make_zero",
            "-movflags", "+faststart",
            str(output)
        ]
        run_ffmpeg(args)
        clips.append(output)

    concat_file = work / "concat.txt"
    concat_file.write_text(
        "".join("file '" + str(x).replace("\\", "/").replace("'", "'\\''") + "'\n"
                for x in clips),
        encoding="utf-8"
    )

    output = VIDEO_DIR / f"ai_edit_{uuid.uuid4().hex}.mp4"

    args = [
        "-f", "concat",
        "-safe", "0",
        "-i", str(concat_file)
    ]

    audio = safe_storage_file(audio_path) if audio_path else None
    if audio:
        args += ["-i", str(audio)]

    subtitle = safe_storage_file(subtitle_path) if subtitle_path else None

    if subtitle:
        escaped = str(subtitle).replace("\\", "/").replace(":", "\\:")
        args += ["-vf", f"subtitles='{escaped}'"]

    if audio:
        args += ["-map", "0:v:0", "-map", "1:a:0", "-shortest"]
    else:
        args += ["-map", "0:v:0", "-map", "0:a?"]

    args += [
        "-c:v", "libx264",
        "-preset", "medium",
        "-crf", "20",
        "-c:a", "aac",
        "-movflags", "+faststart",
        str(output)
    ]

    run_ffmpeg(args)

    return str(output), output.stat().st_size


========== 文件：apps\api\app\services\media_service.py ==========
import json
import os
import subprocess
import urllib.request
import urllib.error
from pathlib import Path
from cryptography.fernet import Fernet

ROOT = Path(os.environ.get("APP_DATA_DIR", str(Path(__file__).resolve().parents[4])))
STORAGE = ROOT / "storage"
VIDEO_DIR = STORAGE / "videos"
AUDIO_DIR = STORAGE / "audio"
SUBTITLE_DIR = STORAGE / "subtitles"

for folder in (VIDEO_DIR, AUDIO_DIR, SUBTITLE_DIR):
    folder.mkdir(parents=True, exist_ok=True)


def decrypt_api_key(value):
    if not value:
        return ""
    key_file = STORAGE / "secrets" / "local.key"
    if not key_file.exists():
        raise RuntimeError("API 密钥加密文件不存在，无法解密已保存的密钥")
    try:
        return Fernet(key_file.read_bytes()).decrypt(value.encode()).decode()
    except Exception as exc:
        raise RuntimeError("API 密钥解密失败，请在 AI 设置中重新保存密钥") from exc


def http_json(url, api_key, method="POST", body=None, timeout=300):
    headers = {
        "Authorization": f"Bearer {api_key}",
        "Content-Type": "application/json",
        "Accept": "application/json",
    }
    data = json.dumps(body or {}, ensure_ascii=False).encode("utf-8")
    request = urllib.request.Request(
        url, data=data if method != "GET" else None,
        headers=headers, method=method
    )
    try:
        with urllib.request.urlopen(request, timeout=timeout) as response:
            raw = response.read()
            content_type = response.headers.get("Content-Type", "")
            if "json" in content_type or raw[:1] in (b"{", b"["):
                return json.loads(raw.decode("utf-8"))
            return {"raw_response": raw.decode("utf-8", errors="replace")}
    except urllib.error.HTTPError as exc:
        detail = exc.read().decode("utf-8", errors="replace")
        raise RuntimeError(f"远程 API 返回 HTTP {exc.code}: {detail[:2000]}") from exc
    except urllib.error.URLError as exc:
        raise RuntimeError(f"无法连接远程 API: {exc}") from exc


def http_download(url, destination, api_key=None):
    headers = {}
    if api_key:
        headers["Authorization"] = f"Bearer {api_key}"
    request = urllib.request.Request(url, headers=headers)
    try:
        with urllib.request.urlopen(request, timeout=600) as response:
            with open(destination, "wb") as output:
                while True:
                    chunk = response.read(1024 * 1024)
                    if not chunk:
                        break
                    output.write(chunk)
    except Exception:
        if os.path.exists(destination):
            os.remove(destination)
        raise


def safe_storage_file(path):
    candidate = Path(path).resolve()
    root = STORAGE.resolve()
    if candidate != root and root not in candidate.parents:
        raise ValueError("文件路径超出素材存储目录")
    if not candidate.is_file():
        raise FileNotFoundError("指定的素材文件不存在")
    return candidate


def run_ffmpeg(args):
    executable = _bundled_media_path("ffmpeg.exe")
    command = [executable, "-y"] + [str(item) for item in args]
    try:
        result = subprocess.run(
            command, capture_output=True, text=True, timeout=7200
        )
    except FileNotFoundError as exc:
        raise RuntimeError("未找到 FFmpeg，请先安装 FFmpeg 并配置 PATH") from exc
    if result.returncode != 0:
        raise RuntimeError("FFmpeg 执行失败：" + result.stderr[-3000:])
    return result


def probe_media(path):
    executable = _bundled_media_path("ffprobe.exe")
    command = [
        executable, "-v", "quiet", "-print_format", "json",
        "-show_format", "-show_streams", str(path)
    ]
    try:
        result = subprocess.run(
            command, capture_output=True, text=True, timeout=120
        )
    except FileNotFoundError as exc:
        raise RuntimeError("未找到 FFprobe，请安装 FFmpeg 完整版本") from exc
    if result.returncode != 0:
        raise RuntimeError(result.stderr[-2000:])
    return json.loads(result.stdout)



# Packaged desktop media-tool discovery.
def _bundled_media_path(filename: str) -> str:
    from pathlib import Path
    import os
    import sys

    configured = os.environ.get(
        "FFMPEG_PATH" if filename == "ffmpeg.exe" else "FFPROBE_PATH"
    )
    if configured and Path(configured).is_file():
        return configured

    candidates = []
    if getattr(sys, "frozen", False):
        candidates.append(
            Path(sys.executable).resolve().parent.parent / "media" / filename
        )
        candidates.append(
            Path(sys.executable).resolve().parent / "media" / filename
        )

    for candidate in candidates:
        if candidate.is_file():
            return str(candidate)

    from shutil import which
    command = "ffmpeg" if filename == "ffmpeg.exe" else "ffprobe"
    found = which(command)
    if found:
        return found

    raise FileNotFoundError(
        f"找不到 {command}。请检查软件媒体组件是否完整安装。"
    )
