========== 文件：apps\desktop\main.cjs ==========
const { app, BrowserWindow, dialog } = require("electron");
const { spawn, execFile } = require("child_process");
const path = require("path");
const fs = require("fs");
const http = require("http");
const net = require("net");

const API_PORT = 8000;
const WEB_PORT = 3000;

let mainWindow = null;
let apiProcess = null;
let webProcess = null;
let quitting = false;
let startupFailed = false;

const gotLock = app.requestSingleInstanceLock();

if (!gotLock) {
  app.quit();
} else {
  app.on("second-instance", () => {
    if (mainWindow) {
      if (mainWindow.isMinimized()) mainWindow.restore();
      mainWindow.focus();
    }
  });
}

function checkPort(port) {
  return new Promise((resolve) => {
    const server = net.createServer();
    server.once("error", () => resolve(false));
    server.once("listening", () => {
      server.close(() => resolve(true));
    });
    server.listen(port, "127.0.0.1");
  });
}

function requestHealth() {
  return new Promise((resolve) => {
    const req = http.get(
      `http://127.0.0.1:${API_PORT}/health`,
      { timeout: 2000 },
      (res) => {
        let body = "";
        res.setEncoding("utf8");
        res.on("data", (chunk) => body += chunk);
        res.on("end", () => {
          try {
            const data = JSON.parse(body);
            resolve(res.statusCode === 200 ? data : null);
          } catch {
            resolve(null);
          }
        });
      }
    );
    req.on("error", () => resolve(null));
    req.on("timeout", () => {
      req.destroy();
      resolve(null);
    });
  });
}

function waitForApi(timeout = 120000) {
  return new Promise((resolve, reject) => {
    const started = Date.now();

    const timer = setInterval(async () => {
      if (apiProcess && apiProcess.exitCode !== null) {
        clearInterval(timer);
        reject(new Error("本地 API 服务提前退出，请检查日志。"));
        return;
      }

      const health = await requestHealth();
      if (health) {
        clearInterval(timer);
        resolve(health);
        return;
      }

      if (Date.now() - started > timeout) {
        clearInterval(timer);
        reject(new Error("等待本地 API 健康检查超时。"));
      }
    }, 800);
  });
}

function waitForWeb(timeout = 120000) {
  return new Promise((resolve, reject) => {
    const started = Date.now();

    const timer = setInterval(() => {
      if (webProcess && webProcess.exitCode !== null) {
        clearInterval(timer);
        reject(new Error("Next.js 服务提前退出，请检查日志。"));
        return;
      }

      const req = http.get("http://127.0.0.1:3000", (res) => {
        res.resume();
        clearInterval(timer);
        resolve();
      });

      req.on("error", () => {
        if (Date.now() - started > timeout) {
          clearInterval(timer);
          reject(new Error("等待前端服务超时。"));
        }
      });
    }, 800);
  });
}

function logChild(name, child) {
  child.on("error", (error) => {
    console.error(`[${name}]`, error);
  });
  child.stdout?.on("data", (data) => {
    console.log(`[${name}] ${data}`);
  });
  child.stderr?.on("data", (data) => {
    console.error(`[${name}] ${data}`);
  });
}

function stopChild(child) {
  if (!child || !child.pid) return;

  if (process.platform === "win32") {
    execFile(
      "taskkill",
      ["/PID", String(child.pid), "/T", "/F"],
      () => {}
    );
  } else {
    try { child.kill("SIGTERM"); } catch {}
  }
}

function stopServices() {
  stopChild(webProcess);
  stopChild(apiProcess);
  webProcess = null;
  apiProcess = null;
}

function startProductionServices() {
  const resources = process.resourcesPath;
  const apiExe = path.join(resources, "api", "ai-comic-api.exe");
  const webServer = path.join(resources, "web", "server.js");
  const webNodeModules = path.join(resources, "web", "node_modules", "next");
  const ffmpeg = path.join(resources, "media", "ffmpeg.exe");
  const ffprobe = path.join(resources, "media", "ffprobe.exe");
  const dataDir = app.getPath("userData");

  for (const file of [apiExe, webServer, webNodeModules, ffmpeg, ffprobe]) {
    if (!fs.existsSync(file)) {
      throw new Error(`安装包缺少必要文件：${file}`);
    }
  }

  fs.mkdirSync(dataDir, { recursive: true });

  if (fs.existsSync(dataDir)) {
    const storageDir = path.join(dataDir, "storage");
    fs.mkdirSync(storageDir, { recursive: true });
  }

  apiProcess = spawn(apiExe, [], {
    cwd: resources,
    windowsHide: true,
    env: {
      ...process.env,
      APP_DATA_DIR: dataDir,
      PYTHONUNBUFFERED: "1",
      FFMPEG_PATH: ffmpeg,
      FFPROBE_PATH: ffprobe
    }
  });

  logChild("API", apiProcess);

  webProcess = spawn(process.execPath, [webServer], {
    cwd: path.join(resources, "web"),
    windowsHide: true,
    env: {
      ...process.env,
      ELECTRON_RUN_AS_NODE: "1",
      PORT: String(WEB_PORT),
      HOSTNAME: "127.0.0.1",
      NEXT_PUBLIC_API_BASE_URL: "http://127.0.0.1:8000"
    }
  });

  logChild("WEB", webProcess);
}

async function createWindow() {
  mainWindow = new BrowserWindow({
    width: 1440,
    height: 900,
    minWidth: 1100,
    minHeight: 700,
    show: false,
    autoHideMenuBar: true,
    webPreferences: {
      preload: path.join(__dirname, "preload.cjs"),
      contextIsolation: true,
      nodeIntegration: false
    }
  });

  mainWindow.once("ready-to-show", () => mainWindow.show());

  if (app.isPackaged) {
    const apiPortFree = await checkPort(API_PORT);
    const webPortFree = await checkPort(WEB_PORT);

    if (!apiPortFree || !webPortFree) {
      throw new Error(
        "软件需要使用本地 8000 和 3000 端口，但端口已被其他程序占用。请关闭占用程序后重新启动。"
      );
    }

    startProductionServices();
    await waitForApi();
    await waitForWeb();
    await mainWindow.loadURL("http://127.0.0.1:3000");
  } else {
    await mainWindow.loadURL("http://127.0.0.1:3000");
  }

  mainWindow.on("closed", () => {
    mainWindow = null;
  });
}

if (gotLock) {
  app.whenReady().then(createWindow).catch(async (error) => {
    startupFailed = true;
    console.error("应用启动失败：", error);
    stopServices();

    await dialog.showMessageBox({
      type: "error",
      title: "AI漫剧制作大师启动失败",
      message: "软件无法正常启动",
      detail: String(error.message || error)
    });

    app.quit();
  });

  app.on("before-quit", () => {
    quitting = true;
    stopServices();
  });

  app.on("window-all-closed", () => {
    if (process.platform !== "darwin") app.quit();
  });

  app.on("activate", () => {
    if (BrowserWindow.getAllWindows().length === 0 && !startupFailed) {
      createWindow().catch(console.error);
    }
  });
}

========== 文件：apps\web\src\app\ai-center\page.tsx ==========
"use client";

import { useEffect, useState } from "react";

const API = process.env.NEXT_PUBLIC_API_BASE_URL || "http://127.0.0.1:8000";

type Provider = {
  id: number;
  provider: string;
  display_name: string;
  base_url: string;
  model: string;
  enabled: boolean;
  paid: boolean;
  purpose: string;
  has_api_key: boolean;
};

const presets = [
  { id: "deepseek", name: "DeepSeek", url: "https://api.deepseek.com", model: "deepseek-chat", paid: true, purpose: "text" },
  { id: "doubao", name: "豆包", url: "https://ark.cn-beijing.volces.com/api/v3", model: "", paid: true, purpose: "image" },
  { id: "openai", name: "OpenAI", url: "https://api.openai.com/v1", model: "gpt-4.1-mini", paid: true, purpose: "text" },
  { id: "grok", name: "Grok", url: "https://api.x.ai/v1", model: "grok-3-mini", paid: true, purpose: "text" },
  { id: "gemini", name: "Google Gemini", url: "https://generativelanguage.googleapis.com/v1beta/openai", model: "gemini-2.5-flash", paid: true, purpose: "text" },
  { id: "cloudflare", name: "Cloudflare Workers AI", url: "", model: "", paid: false, purpose: "text" },
];

export default function AICenterPage() {
  const [providers, setProviders] = useState<Provider[]>([]);
  const [network, setNetwork] = useState(false);
  const [paid, setPaid] = useState(false);
  const [selected, setSelected] = useState(presets[0]);
  const [apiKey, setApiKey] = useState("");
  const [baseUrl, setBaseUrl] = useState(presets[0].url);
  const [model, setModel] = useState(presets[0].model);
  const [enabled, setEnabled] = useState(false);
  const [logs, setLogs] = useState<any[]>([]);
  const [message, setMessage] = useState("");

  async function api(path: string, options?: RequestInit) {
    const response = await fetch(`${API}${path}`, options);
    const data = await response.json().catch(() => ({}));
    if (!response.ok) throw new Error(data.detail || "请求失败");
    return data;
  }

  async function refresh() {
    try {
      const [p, r, l] = await Promise.all([
        api("/api/ai/providers"),
        api("/api/ai/runtime"),
        api("/api/ai/usage-logs"),
      ]);
      setProviders(p.items || []);
      setNetwork(r.network_enabled);
      setPaid(r.paid_api_allowed);
      setLogs(l.items || []);
    } catch (e) {
      setMessage(e instanceof Error ? e.message : "读取失败");
    }
  }

  useEffect(() => { refresh(); }, []);

  function choose(item: typeof presets[number]) {
    setSelected(item);
    setBaseUrl(item.url);
    setModel(item.model);
    setApiKey("");
    const old = providers.find((x) => x.provider === item.id);
    setEnabled(old?.enabled || false);
  }

  async function save() {
    try {
      await api("/api/ai/providers", {
        method: "POST",
        headers: { "Content-Type": "application/json" },
        body: JSON.stringify({
          provider: selected.id,
          display_name: selected.name,
          base_url: baseUrl,
          model,
          api_key: apiKey,
          enabled,
          paid: selected.paid,
          purpose: selected.purpose,
        }),
      });
      setApiKey("");
      await refresh();
      setMessage(`${selected.name} 配置已保存`);
    } catch (e) {
      setMessage(e instanceof Error ? e.message : "保存失败");
    }
  }

  async function saveRuntime(nextNetwork: boolean, nextPaid: boolean) {
    try {
      await api("/api/ai/runtime", {
        method: "PUT",
        headers: { "Content-Type": "application/json" },
        body: JSON.stringify({
          network_enabled: nextNetwork,
          paid_api_allowed: nextPaid,
        }),
      });
      setNetwork(nextNetwork);
      setPaid(nextPaid);
      setMessage("调用权限设置已保存");
    } catch (e) {
      setMessage(e instanceof Error ? e.message : "设置失败");
    }
  }

  async function removeProvider() {
    if (!confirm(`确定删除 ${selected.name} 配置吗？`)) return;
    try {
      await api(`/api/ai/providers/${selected.id}`, { method: "DELETE" });
      await refresh();
      setMessage("配置已删除");
    } catch (e) {
      setMessage(e instanceof Error ? e.message : "删除失败");
    }
  }

  return (
    <main className="cosmic min-h-screen text-white">
      <div className="nebula nebula-one" />
      <div className="nebula nebula-two" />
      <div className="relative z-10 min-h-screen p-5 lg:p-8">
        <header className="mb-7">
          <a href="/" className="text-sm text-purple-300">← 返回首页</a>
          <h1 className="mt-3 text-3xl font-black">⚙️ AI 模型管理中心</h1>
          <p className="mt-2 text-sm text-purple-200">配置 AI 服务、控制网络权限、管理调用记录</p>
        </header>

        {message && <div className="mb-5 rounded-xl border border-purple-400/30 bg-purple-950/70 p-3 text-sm">{message}</div>}

        <section className="mb-5 grid grid-cols-1 gap-4 md:grid-cols-2">
          <div className="rounded-2xl border border-purple-400/20 bg-[#160d2acc] p-5">
            <h2 className="font-bold">网络调用总开关</h2>
            <p className="my-2 text-xs text-purple-300">关闭后，所有外部 AI 请求都会被阻止。</p>
            <button onClick={() => saveRuntime(!network, paid)} className={`rounded-lg px-5 py-2 text-sm font-bold ${network ? "bg-emerald-700" : "bg-gray-700"}`}>
              {network ? "已开启" : "已关闭"}
            </button>
          </div>
          <div className="rounded-2xl border border-purple-400/20 bg-[#160d2acc] p-5">
            <h2 className="font-bold">付费 API 总授权</h2>
            <p className="my-2 text-xs text-purple-300">开启仅表示允许付费请求，还必须在具体请求时再次确认。</p>
            <button onClick={() => saveRuntime(network, !paid)} className={`rounded-lg px-5 py-2 text-sm font-bold ${paid ? "bg-amber-700" : "bg-gray-700"}`}>
              {paid ? "已授权" : "未授权"}
            </button>
          </div>
        </section>

        <section className="grid grid-cols-1 gap-5 xl:grid-cols-[300px_minmax(0,1fr)]">
          <aside className="rounded-2xl border border-purple-400/20 bg-[#160d2acc] p-5">
            <h2 className="mb-4 text-lg font-bold">AI 服务商</h2>
            <div className="space-y-2">
              {presets.map((p) => {
                const saved = providers.find((x) => x.provider === p.id);
                return (
                  <button key={p.id} onClick={() => choose(p)} className={`w-full rounded-xl border p-3 text-left ${selected.id === p.id ? "border-purple-300 bg-purple-700/30" : "border-purple-400/15 bg-black/20"}`}>
                    <div className="flex justify-between gap-2">
                      <span className="font-semibold">{p.name}</span>
                      <span className={`text-xs ${saved?.enabled ? "text-emerald-300" : "text-gray-400"}`}>{saved?.enabled ? "已启用" : "未启用"}</span>
                    </div>
                    <div className="mt-1 text-xs text-purple-300">{p.purpose === "image" ? "视觉生成" : "文本与分析"} · {p.paid ? "付费服务" : "免费额度/需核实"}</div>
                  </button>
                );
              })}
            </div>
          </aside>

          <div className="space-y-5">
            <section className="rounded-2xl border border-purple-400/20 bg-[#160d2acc] p-5">
              <h2 className="mb-5 text-xl font-bold">{selected.name} 配置</h2>
              <div className="grid grid-cols-1 gap-4 md:grid-cols-2">
                <label className="text-sm text-purple-200">API 地址
                  <input value={baseUrl} onChange={(e) => setBaseUrl(e.target.value)} className="mt-2 w-full rounded-lg border border-purple-400/30 bg-black/30 p-3 text-white" />
                </label>
                <label className="text-sm text-purple-200">模型名称
                  <input value={model} onChange={(e) => setModel(e.target.value)} className="mt-2 w-full rounded-lg border border-purple-400/30 bg-black/30 p-3 text-white" />
                </label>
                <label className="text-sm text-purple-200 md:col-span-2">API 密钥（留空则保留已保存的密钥）
                  <input type="password" value={apiKey} onChange={(e) => setApiKey(e.target.value)} placeholder="输入 API Key" className="mt-2 w-full rounded-lg border border-purple-400/30 bg-black/30 p-3 text-white" />
                </label>
              </div>
              <label className="mt-4 flex items-center gap-3 text-sm">
                <input type="checkbox" checked={enabled} onChange={(e) => setEnabled(e.target.checked)} />
                启用此服务商
              </label>
              <div className="mt-5 flex flex-wrap gap-3">
                <button onClick={save} className="rounded-lg bg-purple-600 px-5 py-2 font-semibold">保存配置</button>
                <button onClick={removeProvider} className="rounded-lg border border-red-400/30 px-5 py-2 text-red-300">删除配置</button>
              </div>
              <p className="mt-4 text-xs leading-6 text-purple-300">
                密钥保存在本机加密文件中，接口不会返回密钥明文。不同服务商的模型名称和 API 地址请以官方文档为准。豆包视觉生成后续使用独立图片生成适配器。
              </p>
            </section>

            <section className="rounded-2xl border border-purple-400/20 bg-[#160d2acc] p-5">
              <h2 className="mb-4 text-lg font-bold">最近调用记录</h2>
              <div className="max-h-72 space-y-2 overflow-y-auto">
                {logs.map((log) => (
                  <div key={log.id} className="flex flex-wrap justify-between gap-2 rounded-lg bg-black/20 p-3 text-xs">
                    <span>{log.provider} · {log.model} · {log.task}</span>
                    <span className={log.status === "success" ? "text-emerald-300" : "text-red-300"}>{log.status}</span>
                  </div>
                ))}
                {logs.length === 0 && <p className="text-sm text-purple-300">暂无调用记录。</p>}
              </div>
            </section>
          </div>
        </section>
      </div>
    </main>
  );
}


========== 文件：apps\web\src\app\assets\page.tsx ==========
"use client";

import { useEffect, useState } from "react";

const API = process.env.NEXT_PUBLIC_API_BASE_URL || "http://127.0.0.1:8000";

type Asset = {
  id: number;
  filename: string;
  category: string;
  mime_type: string;
  file_size: number;
  description: string;
  url: string;
};

const categories = [
  ["image", "图片素材"],
  ["audio", "音频素材"],
  ["video", "视频素材"],
  ["other", "其他文件"],
];

export default function AssetsPage() {
  const [assets, setAssets] = useState<Asset[]>([]);
  const [category, setCategory] = useState("all");
  const [uploadCategory, setUploadCategory] = useState("image");
  const [file, setFile] = useState<File | null>(null);
  const [description, setDescription] = useState("");
  const [message, setMessage] = useState("");
  const [loading, setLoading] = useState(false);

  async function refresh() {
    const response = await fetch(`${API}/api/assets${category === "all" ? "" : `?category=${category}`}`);
    const data = await response.json();
    if (!response.ok) throw new Error(data.detail || "读取素材失败");
    setAssets(data.items || []);
  }

  useEffect(() => {
    refresh().catch((e) => setMessage(e.message));
  }, [category]);

  async function upload() {
    if (!file) {
      setMessage("请先选择文件");
      return;
    }
    setLoading(true);
    try {
      const form = new FormData();
      form.append("file", file);
      form.append("category", uploadCategory);
      form.append("description", description);
      const response = await fetch(`${API}/api/assets/upload`, { method: "POST", body: form });
      const data = await response.json();
      if (!response.ok) throw new Error(data.detail || "上传失败");
      setFile(null);
      setDescription("");
      setMessage("素材上传成功");
      await refresh();
    } catch (e) {
      setMessage(e instanceof Error ? e.message : "上传失败");
    } finally {
      setLoading(false);
    }
  }

  async function remove(id: number) {
    if (!confirm("确定删除这份素材吗？")) return;
    const response = await fetch(`${API}/api/assets/${id}`, { method: "DELETE" });
    if (!response.ok) {
      setMessage("删除失败");
      return;
    }
    await refresh();
  }

  function sizeText(size: number) {
    if (size >= 1024 ** 3) return `${(size / 1024 ** 3).toFixed(2)} GB`;
    if (size >= 1024 ** 2) return `${(size / 1024 ** 2).toFixed(2)} MB`;
    if (size >= 1024) return `${(size / 1024).toFixed(1)} KB`;
    return `${size} B`;
  }

  return (
    <main className="cosmic min-h-screen text-white">
      <div className="nebula nebula-one" />
      <div className="nebula nebula-two" />
      <div className="relative z-10 min-h-screen p-5 lg:p-8">
        <header className="mb-7">
          <a href="/" className="text-sm text-purple-300">← 返回首页</a>
          <h1 className="mt-3 text-3xl font-black">🗄️ 统一素材库</h1>
          <p className="mt-2 text-sm text-purple-200">集中管理人物图、场景图、道具图、音频、视频及其他文件</p>
        </header>

        {message && <div className="mb-5 rounded-xl border border-purple-400/30 bg-purple-950/70 p-3 text-sm">{message}</div>}

        <section className="mb-5 rounded-2xl border border-purple-400/20 bg-[#160d2acc] p-5">
          <h2 className="mb-4 text-lg font-bold">上传素材</h2>
          <div className="grid grid-cols-1 gap-3 md:grid-cols-2">
            <input type="file" onChange={(e) => setFile(e.target.files?.[0] || null)} className="rounded-lg border border-purple-400/30 bg-black/30 p-3 text-sm" />
            <select value={uploadCategory} onChange={(e) => setUploadCategory(e.target.value)} className="rounded-lg border border-purple-400/30 bg-[#211338] p-3">
              {categories.map(([id, name]) => <option key={id} value={id}>{name}</option>)}
            </select>
            <input value={description} onChange={(e) => setDescription(e.target.value)} placeholder="素材说明（可选）" className="rounded-lg border border-purple-400/30 bg-black/30 p-3 md:col-span-2" />
          </div>
          <button onClick={upload} disabled={loading} className="mt-4 rounded-lg bg-purple-600 px-6 py-2 font-semibold disabled:opacity-50">
            {loading ? "上传中..." : "上传到本地素材库"}
          </button>
          <p className="mt-3 text-xs text-purple-300">文件保存在本地 storage/assets 目录。当前单文件上限为 2 GB。</p>
        </section>

        <section className="rounded-2xl border border-purple-400/20 bg-[#160d2acc] p-5">
          <div className="mb-5 flex flex-wrap items-center justify-between gap-3">
            <h2 className="text-lg font-bold">素材列表</h2>
            <select value={category} onChange={(e) => setCategory(e.target.value)} className="rounded-lg border border-purple-400/30 bg-[#211338] px-4 py-2">
              <option value="all">全部素材</option>
              {categories.map(([id, name]) => <option key={id} value={id}>{name}</option>)}
            </select>
          </div>
          <div className="grid grid-cols-1 gap-3 md:grid-cols-2 xl:grid-cols-3">
            {assets.map((asset) => (
              <article key={asset.id} className="rounded-xl border border-purple-400/20 bg-black/20 p-4">
                <div className="text-2xl">{asset.category === "image" ? "🖼️" : asset.category === "audio" ? "🎵" : asset.category === "video" ? "🎬" : "📦"}</div>
                <h3 className="mt-3 break-all font-semibold">{asset.filename}</h3>
                <p className="mt-1 text-xs text-purple-300">{sizeText(asset.file_size)} · {asset.category}</p>
                <p className="mt-2 break-words text-xs text-purple-200">{asset.description}</p>
                <div className="mt-4 flex gap-4 text-sm">
                  <a href={`${API}${asset.url}`} target="_blank" rel="noreferrer" className="text-purple-300">打开文件</a>
                  <button onClick={() => remove(asset.id)} className="text-red-300">删除</button>
                </div>
              </article>
            ))}
            {assets.length === 0 && <p className="text-sm text-purple-300">当前分类还没有素材。</p>}
          </div>
        </section>
      </div>
    </main>
  );
}

========== 文件：apps\web\src\app\media-studio\page.tsx ==========
"use client";

import { useEffect, useState } from "react";

const API = process.env.NEXT_PUBLIC_API_BASE_URL || "http://127.0.0.1:8000";

type MediaTask = {
  id: number;
  provider: string;
  model: string;
  status: string;
  prompt: string;
  remote_task_id: string;
  result_path: string;
  error: string;
};

export default function MediaStudioPage() {
  const [provider, setProvider] = useState("openai");
  const [model, setModel] = useState("");
  const [prompt, setPrompt] = useState("");
  const [duration, setDuration] = useState(10);
  const [confirmPaid, setConfirmPaid] = useState(false);
  const [tasks, setTasks] = useState<MediaTask[]>([]);
  const [speechText, setSpeechText] = useState("");
  const [speechModel, setSpeechModel] = useState("");
  const [voice, setVoice] = useState("alloy");
  const [message, setMessage] = useState("");

  async function loadTasks() {
    try {
      const response = await fetch(`${API}/api/media/video/tasks`);
      if (response.ok) setTasks(await response.json());
    } catch {}
  }

  useEffect(() => { loadTasks(); }, []);

  async function createVideo() {
    setMessage("正在提交真实视频生成请求……");
    try {
      const response = await fetch(`${API}/api/media/video/tasks`, {
        method: "POST",
        headers: { "Content-Type": "application/json" },
        body: JSON.stringify({
          provider, model, prompt, duration,
          confirm_paid: confirmPaid
        })
      });
      const data = await response.json();
      if (!response.ok) throw new Error(data.detail || "视频任务提交失败");
      setMessage(`视频任务已提交，远程任务编号：${data.remote_task_id}`);
      await loadTasks();
    } catch (error) {
      setMessage(error instanceof Error ? error.message : "请求失败");
    }
  }

  async function refreshTask(id: number) {
    try {
      const response = await fetch(`${API}/api/media/video/tasks/${id}/refresh`, {
        method: "POST"
      });
      const data = await response.json();
      if (!response.ok) throw new Error(data.detail || "查询失败");
      setMessage(`任务状态：${data.status}`);
      await loadTasks();
    } catch (error) {
      setMessage(error instanceof Error ? error.message : "查询失败");
    }
  }

  async function generateSpeech() {
    setMessage("正在调用真实语音生成 API……");
    try {
      const response = await fetch(`${API}/api/media/speech/generate`, {
        method: "POST",
        headers: { "Content-Type": "application/json" },
        body: JSON.stringify({
          provider, model: speechModel, text: speechText,
          voice, confirm_paid: confirmPaid
        })
      });
      const data = await response.json();
      if (!response.ok) throw new Error(data.detail || "配音失败");
      setMessage(`配音完成：${data.file}`);
    } catch (error) {
      setMessage(error instanceof Error ? error.message : "配音失败");
    }
  }

  return (
    <main className="min-h-screen p-8 text-white">
      <div className="mx-auto max-w-6xl space-y-8">
        <header>
          <h1 className="text-3xl font-bold">AI 视频与音频生产中心</h1>
          <p className="mt-2 text-white/60">视频生成、AI 配音、任务查询与媒体合成</p>
        </header>

        {message && <div className="rounded-xl border border-purple-400/30 bg-purple-500/10 p-4">{message}</div>}

        <section className="grid gap-6 lg:grid-cols-2">
          <div className="rounded-2xl border border-purple-300/20 bg-slate-950/65 p-6">
            <h2 className="mb-5 text-xl font-semibold">真实 AI 视频生成</h2>
            <label className="mb-2 block text-sm">服务商 ID</label>
            <input value={provider} onChange={e => setProvider(e.target.value)}
              className="mb-4 w-full rounded-lg border border-white/15 bg-black/30 p-3" />
            <label className="mb-2 block text-sm">视频模型名称</label>
            <input value={model} onChange={e => setModel(e.target.value)}
              placeholder="填写服务商提供的视频模型 ID"
              className="mb-4 w-full rounded-lg border border-white/15 bg-black/30 p-3" />
            <label className="mb-2 block text-sm">视频描述</label>
            <textarea value={prompt} onChange={e => setPrompt(e.target.value)}
              rows={5} className="mb-4 w-full rounded-lg border border-white/15 bg-black/30 p-3"
              placeholder="描述人物动作、场景、镜头运动和画面风格" />
            <label className="mb-2 block text-sm">视频时长（秒）</label>
            <input type="number" min={1} max={600} value={duration}
              onChange={e => setDuration(Number(e.target.value))}
              className="mb-4 w-full rounded-lg border border-white/15 bg-black/30 p-3" />
            <label className="mb-4 flex items-center gap-2 text-sm">
              <input type="checkbox" checked={confirmPaid}
                onChange={e => setConfirmPaid(e.target.checked)} />
              我已确认本次请求可能产生 API 费用
            </label>
            <button onClick={createVideo}
              className="rounded-xl bg-purple-600 px-6 py-3 font-semibold hover:bg-purple-500">
              提交视频生成
            </button>
          </div>

          <div className="rounded-2xl border border-purple-300/20 bg-slate-950/65 p-6">
            <h2 className="mb-5 text-xl font-semibold">AI 配音</h2>
            <label className="mb-2 block text-sm">服务商 ID</label>
            <input value={provider} onChange={e => setProvider(e.target.value)}
              className="mb-4 w-full rounded-lg border border-white/15 bg-black/30 p-3" />
            <label className="mb-2 block text-sm">语音模型名称</label>
            <input value={speechModel} onChange={e => setSpeechModel(e.target.value)}
              placeholder="填写已开通的语音模型 ID"
              className="mb-4 w-full rounded-lg border border-white/15 bg-black/30 p-3" />
            <label className="mb-2 block text-sm">声音 ID</label>
            <input value={voice} onChange={e => setVoice(e.target.value)}
              className="mb-4 w-full rounded-lg border border-white/15 bg-black/30 p-3" />
            <label className="mb-2 block text-sm">配音文本</label>
            <textarea value={speechText} onChange={e => setSpeechText(e.target.value)}
              rows={5} className="mb-4 w-full rounded-lg border border-white/15 bg-black/30 p-3"
              placeholder="输入需要生成语音的台词或旁白" />
            <button onClick={generateSpeech}
              className="rounded-xl bg-indigo-600 px-6 py-3 font-semibold hover:bg-indigo-500">
              生成配音
            </button>
          </div>
        </section>

        <section className="rounded-2xl border border-purple-300/20 bg-slate-950/65 p-6">
          <div className="mb-5 flex items-center justify-between">
            <h2 className="text-xl font-semibold">视频任务队列</h2>
            <button onClick={loadTasks} className="rounded-lg border border-white/20 px-4 py-2">
              刷新列表
            </button>
          </div>
          <div className="space-y-3">
            {tasks.map(task => (
              <div key={task.id} className="rounded-xl border border-white/10 bg-black/20 p-4">
                <div className="flex flex-wrap items-center justify-between gap-3">
                  <div>
                    <div className="font-semibold">任务 #{task.id} · {task.provider}</div>
                    <div className="mt-1 text-sm text-white/60">{task.model} · {task.status}</div>
                  </div>
                  <button onClick={() => refreshTask(task.id)}
                    className="rounded-lg bg-purple-700 px-4 py-2">查询任务</button>
                </div>
                <p className="mt-3 text-sm text-white/70">{task.prompt}</p>
                {task.result_path && <p className="mt-2 break-all text-xs text-green-300">{task.result_path}</p>}
                {task.error && <p className="mt-2 text-sm text-red-300">{task.error}</p>}
              </div>
            ))}
            {tasks.length === 0 && <p className="text-white/50">暂无视频任务</p>}
          </div>
        </section>
      </div>
    </main>
  );
}


========== 文件：apps\web\src\app\novels\page.tsx ==========
"use client";

import { useEffect, useRef, useState } from "react";

const API = process.env.NEXT_PUBLIC_API_BASE_URL || "http://127.0.0.1:8000";

type Novel = {
  id: number;
  title: string;
  description: string;
  genre: string;
  status: string;
  chapter_count: number;
  word_count: number;
};

type Chapter = {
  id: number;
  chapter_number: number;
  title: string;
  content: string;
  word_count: number;
};

type AnalysisItem = {
  id: number;
  category: string;
  name: string;
  description: string;
  confirmed: boolean;
};

const categoryNames: Record<string, string> = {
  character: "人物",
  scene: "场景",
  prop: "道具",
  event: "故事事件",
  relationship: "人物关系",
  world: "世界观",
};

export default function NovelsPage() {
  const [novels, setNovels] = useState<Novel[]>([]);
  const [selected, setSelected] = useState<Novel | null>(null);
  const [chapters, setChapters] = useState<Chapter[]>([]);
  const [currentChapter, setCurrentChapter] = useState<Chapter | null>(null);
  const [analysis, setAnalysis] = useState<AnalysisItem[]>([]);
  const [title, setTitle] = useState("");
  const [genre, setGenre] = useState("奇幻");
  const [loading, setLoading] = useState(false);
  const [message, setMessage] = useState("");
  const fileRef = useRef<HTMLInputElement>(null);

  async function request(path: string, options?: RequestInit) {
    const response = await fetch(`${API}${path}`, options);
    const data = await response.json().catch(() => ({}));
    if (!response.ok) throw new Error(data.detail || "请求失败");
    return data;
  }

  async function loadNovels() {
    try {
      const data = await request("/api/novels");
      setNovels(data.items || []);
    } catch (error) {
      setMessage(error instanceof Error ? error.message : "无法连接后端");
    }
  }

  async function openNovel(novel: Novel) {
    setSelected(novel);
    setCurrentChapter(null);
    try {
      const [chapterData, analysisData] = await Promise.all([
        request(`/api/novels/${novel.id}/chapters`),
        request(`/api/novels/${novel.id}/analysis`),
      ]);
      setChapters(chapterData.items || []);
      setAnalysis(analysisData.items || []);
    } catch (error) {
      setMessage(error instanceof Error ? error.message : "读取失败");
    }
  }

  useEffect(() => {
    loadNovels();
  }, []);

  async function createNovel() {
    if (!title.trim()) {
      setMessage("请先填写小说名称");
      return;
    }
    setLoading(true);
    try {
      const item = await request("/api/novels", {
        method: "POST",
        headers: { "Content-Type": "application/json" },
        body: JSON.stringify({ title: title.trim(), genre }),
      });
      setTitle("");
      await loadNovels();
      await openNovel(item);
      setMessage("小说项目创建成功");
    } catch (error) {
      setMessage(error instanceof Error ? error.message : "创建失败");
    } finally {
      setLoading(false);
    }
  }

  async function importTxt() {
    const file = fileRef.current?.files?.[0];
    if (!selected || !file) {
      setMessage("请先选择小说，再选择 TXT 文件");
      return;
    }
    setLoading(true);
    try {
      const form = new FormData();
      form.append("file", file);
      const data = await request(`/api/novels/${selected.id}/import-txt`, {
        method: "POST",
        body: form,
      });
      setChapters(data.chapters || []);
      setCurrentChapter(null);
      await loadNovels();
      setMessage(`导入成功：${data.chapter_count} 章，共 ${data.word_count} 字`);
      if (fileRef.current) fileRef.current.value = "";
    } catch (error) {
      setMessage(error instanceof Error ? error.message : "导入失败");
    } finally {
      setLoading(false);
    }
  }

  async function saveChapter() {
    if (!currentChapter) return;
    setLoading(true);
    try {
      await request(`/api/chapters/${currentChapter.id}`, {
        method: "PUT",
        headers: { "Content-Type": "application/json" },
        body: JSON.stringify({
          title: currentChapter.title,
          content: currentChapter.content,
        }),
      });
      await openNovel(selected!);
      setMessage("章节保存成功");
    } catch (error) {
      setMessage(error instanceof Error ? error.message : "保存失败");
    } finally {
      setLoading(false);
    }
  }

  async function analyze() {
    if (!selected) return;
    setLoading(true);
    try {
      const data = await request(`/api/novels/${selected.id}/analyze`, {
        method: "POST",
      });
      const result = await request(`/api/novels/${selected.id}/analysis`);
      setAnalysis(result.items || []);
      setMessage(data.message || "初步解析完成");
    } catch (error) {
      setMessage(error instanceof Error ? error.message : "解析失败");
    } finally {
      setLoading(false);
    }
  }

  async function confirmAnalysis() {
    if (!selected) return;
    try {
      await request(`/api/novels/${selected.id}/analysis/confirm`, {
        method: "POST",
      });
      const result = await request(`/api/novels/${selected.id}/analysis`);
      setAnalysis(result.items || []);
      setMessage("解析结果已确认并保存");
    } catch (error) {
      setMessage(error instanceof Error ? error.message : "确认失败");
    }
  }

  async function removeNovel() {
    if (!selected || !window.confirm("确定删除这部小说及其章节和解析数据吗？")) return;
    try {
      await request(`/api/novels/${selected.id}`, { method: "DELETE" });
      setSelected(null);
      setChapters([]);
      setAnalysis([]);
      await loadNovels();
      setMessage("小说已删除");
    } catch (error) {
      setMessage(error instanceof Error ? error.message : "删除失败");
    }
  }

  async function editAnalysis(item: AnalysisItem) {
    const next = window.prompt("修改名称", item.name);
    if (next === null || !next.trim()) return;
    try {
      await request(`/api/analysis/${item.id}`, {
        method: "PUT",
        headers: { "Content-Type": "application/json" },
        body: JSON.stringify({ name: next.trim() }),
      });
      const result = await request(`/api/novels/${selected!.id}/analysis`);
      setAnalysis(result.items || []);
    } catch (error) {
      setMessage(error instanceof Error ? error.message : "修改失败");
    }
  }

  async function deleteAnalysis(id: number) {
    try {
      await request(`/api/analysis/${id}`, { method: "DELETE" });
      setAnalysis((items) => items.filter((item) => item.id !== id));
    } catch (error) {
      setMessage(error instanceof Error ? error.message : "删除失败");
    }
  }

  return (
    <main className="cosmic min-h-screen text-white">
      <div className="nebula nebula-one" />
      <div className="nebula nebula-two" />
      <div className="main-layout relative z-10 min-h-screen p-5 lg:p-8">
        <header className="mb-7 flex flex-wrap items-center justify-between gap-4">
          <div>
            <a href="/" className="text-sm text-purple-300 hover:text-white">← 返回首页</a>
            <h1 className="mt-3 text-3xl font-black">📚 小说创作工作室</h1>
            <p className="mt-2 text-sm text-purple-200">小说管理、TXT导入、章节编辑与内容解析</p>
          </div>
          <div className="rounded-full border border-purple-400/30 bg-purple-950/60 px-4 py-2 text-sm">
            本地数据库 · 自动保存项目数据
          </div>
        </header>

        {message && (
          <div className="mb-5 rounded-xl border border-purple-400/30 bg-purple-950/70 p-3 text-sm text-purple-100">
            {message}
          </div>
        )}

        <div className="grid grid-cols-1 gap-5 xl:grid-cols-[340px_minmax(0,1fr)]">
          <aside className="rounded-2xl border border-purple-400/20 bg-[#160d2acc] p-5">
            <h2 className="mb-4 text-lg font-bold">小说项目</h2>
            <div className="space-y-3">
              <input
                value={title}
                onChange={(e) => setTitle(e.target.value)}
                placeholder="输入小说名称"
                className="w-full rounded-xl border border-purple-400/30 bg-black/30 px-4 py-3 outline-none focus:border-purple-300"
              />
              <select
                value={genre}
                onChange={(e) => setGenre(e.target.value)}
                className="w-full rounded-xl border border-purple-400/30 bg-[#211338] px-4 py-3"
              >
                {["奇幻", "玄幻", "都市", "言情", "科幻", "悬疑", "历史", "武侠", "其他"].map((x) => (
                  <option key={x}>{x}</option>
                ))}
              </select>
              <button
                onClick={createNovel}
                disabled={loading}
                className="w-full rounded-xl bg-gradient-to-r from-purple-600 to-fuchsia-600 px-4 py-3 font-bold disabled:opacity-50"
              >
                ＋ 新建小说项目
              </button>
            </div>

            <div className="mb-3 mt-7 flex items-center justify-between">
              <h3 className="font-semibold">我的小说</h3>
              <button onClick={loadNovels} className="text-xs text-purple-300">刷新</button>
            </div>
            <div className="space-y-2">
              {novels.map((novel) => (
                <button
                  key={novel.id}
                  onClick={() => openNovel(novel)}
                  className={`w-full rounded-xl border p-3 text-left transition ${
                    selected?.id === novel.id
                      ? "border-purple-300 bg-purple-700/30"
                      : "border-purple-400/15 bg-black/20 hover:bg-purple-900/40"
                  }`}
                >
                  <div className="font-semibold">{novel.title}</div>
                  <div className="mt-2 text-xs text-purple-300">
                    {novel.genre} · {novel.chapter_count} 章 · {novel.word_count} 字
                  </div>
                </button>
              ))}
              {novels.length === 0 && (
                <p className="rounded-xl bg-black/20 p-4 text-sm text-purple-300">还没有小说，先创建一个项目。</p>
              )}
            </div>
          </aside>

          <section className="min-w-0 space-y-5">
            {!selected ? (
              <div className="flex min-h-[430px] flex-col items-center justify-center rounded-2xl border border-purple-400/20 bg-[#160d2acc] p-8 text-center">
                <div className="text-6xl">📖</div>
                <h2 className="mt-5 text-2xl font-bold">开始你的小说创作</h2>
                <p className="mt-3 max-w-lg text-sm leading-7 text-purple-200">
                  创建小说项目后，可以导入 TXT、管理章节、编辑内容，并进行人物、场景、道具和剧情线索的初步识别。
                </p>
              </div>
            ) : (
              <>
                <div className="rounded-2xl border border-purple-400/20 bg-[#160d2acc] p-5">
                  <div className="flex flex-wrap items-center justify-between gap-3">
                    <div>
                      <h2 className="text-2xl font-bold">{selected.title}</h2>
                      <p className="mt-1 text-sm text-purple-300">{selected.genre} · {chapters.length} 个章节</p>
                    </div>
                    <button onClick={removeNovel} className="rounded-lg border border-red-400/30 px-3 py-2 text-sm text-red-300">
                      删除项目
                    </button>
                  </div>
                  <div className="mt-5 flex flex-wrap items-center gap-3">
                    <input ref={fileRef} type="file" accept=".txt,text/plain" className="max-w-full text-sm text-purple-200" />
                    <button onClick={importTxt} disabled={loading} className="rounded-xl bg-purple-600 px-4 py-2 text-sm font-semibold disabled:opacity-50">
                      导入 TXT 小说
                    </button>
                    <button onClick={analyze} disabled={loading || chapters.length === 0} className="rounded-xl bg-fuchsia-600 px-4 py-2 text-sm font-semibold disabled:opacity-50">
                      ✨ 初步解析小说
                    </button>
                  </div>
                </div>

                <div className="grid grid-cols-1 gap-5 2xl:grid-cols-2">
                  <div className="rounded-2xl border border-purple-400/20 bg-[#160d2acc] p-5">
                    <h3 className="mb-4 text-lg font-bold">章节目录</h3>
                    <div className="max-h-[460px] space-y-2 overflow-y-auto">
                      {chapters.map((chapter) => (
                        <button
                          key={chapter.id}
                          onClick={() => setCurrentChapter(chapter)}
                          className={`w-full rounded-lg border p-3 text-left ${
                            currentChapter?.id === chapter.id
                              ? "border-purple-300 bg-purple-700/30"
                              : "border-purple-400/10 bg-black/20 hover:bg-purple-900/30"
                          }`}
                        >
                          <div className="text-sm font-medium">{chapter.chapter_number}. {chapter.title}</div>
                          <div className="mt-1 text-xs text-purple-400">{chapter.word_count} 字</div>
                        </button>
                      ))}
                      {chapters.length === 0 && <p className="text-sm text-purple-300">导入 TXT 后自动建立章节目录。</p>}
                    </div>
                  </div>

                  <div className="rounded-2xl border border-purple-400/20 bg-[#160d2acc] p-5">
                    <h3 className="mb-4 text-lg font-bold">章节内容编辑</h3>
                    {currentChapter ? (
                      <div className="space-y-3">
                        <input
                          value={currentChapter.title}
                          onChange={(e) => setCurrentChapter({ ...currentChapter, title: e.target.value })}
                          className="w-full rounded-lg border border-purple-400/30 bg-black/30 p-3 font-semibold outline-none"
                        />
                        <textarea
                          value={currentChapter.content}
                          onChange={(e) => setCurrentChapter({ ...currentChapter, content: e.target.value })}
                          className="min-h-[320px] w-full resize-y rounded-lg border border-purple-400/30 bg-black/30 p-3 text-sm leading-7 outline-none"
                        />
                        <button onClick={saveChapter} disabled={loading} className="rounded-lg bg-purple-600 px-5 py-2 font-semibold">
                          保存章节
                        </button>
                      </div>
                    ) : (
                      <p className="py-12 text-center text-sm text-purple-300">从左侧选择章节，查看和编辑正文。</p>
                    )}
                  </div>
                </div>

                <div className="rounded-2xl border border-purple-400/20 bg-[#160d2acc] p-5">
                  <div className="flex flex-wrap items-center justify-between gap-3">
                    <div>
                      <h3 className="text-lg font-bold">🔎 小说解析结果</h3>
                      <p className="mt-1 text-xs text-purple-300">先进行基础线索提取，后续接入 AI 模型进行深度分析。</p>
                    </div>
                    <button onClick={confirmAnalysis} disabled={analysis.length === 0} className="rounded-lg bg-emerald-700 px-4 py-2 text-sm font-semibold disabled:opacity-40">
                      确认全部结果
                    </button>
                  </div>
                  <div className="mt-4 grid grid-cols-1 gap-3 md:grid-cols-2">
                    {analysis.map((item) => (
                      <div key={item.id} className="rounded-xl border border-purple-400/20 bg-black/20 p-4">
                        <div className="flex items-center justify-between gap-2">
                          <span className="text-xs text-purple-300">{categoryNames[item.category] || item.category}</span>
                          <span className={`text-xs ${item.confirmed ? "text-emerald-300" : "text-yellow-300"}`}>
                            {item.confirmed ? "已确认" : "待确认"}
                          </span>
                        </div>
                        <div className="mt-2 font-semibold">{item.name}</div>
                        <p className="mt-1 text-xs leading-5 text-purple-300">{item.description}</p>
                        <div className="mt-3 flex gap-3 text-xs">
                          <button onClick={() => editAnalysis(item)} className="text-purple-200">修改</button>
                          <button onClick={() => deleteAnalysis(item.id)} className="text-red-300">删除</button>
                        </div>
                      </div>
                    ))}
                    {analysis.length === 0 && (
                      <p className="py-5 text-sm text-purple-300">尚无解析结果。导入小说后点击“初步解析小说”。</p>
                    )}
                  </div>
                </div>
              </>
            )}
          </section>
        </div>
      </div>
    </main>
  );
}
