========== 文件：apps\web\src\app\storyboard\page.tsx ==========
"use client";

import { useEffect, useState } from "react";

const API = process.env.NEXT_PUBLIC_API_BASE_URL || "http://127.0.0.1:8000";

type Novel = { id: number; title?: string; name?: string };
type Project = { id: number; novel_id: number; name: string; adaptation_style: string; status: string };
type Episode = {
  id: number; episode_number: number; title: string; source_text: string;
  synopsis: string; screenplay: string; duration_seconds: number; status: string;
};
type Shot = {
  id: number; shot_number: number; scene_name: string; shot_type: string;
  camera_movement: string; duration_seconds: number; visual_description: string;
  character_actions: string; dialogue: string; sound_effects: string; image_prompt: string;
};
type VisualAsset = { id: number; category: string; name: string; description: string };

const styles = [
  "3D动漫电影风格", "真人电影风格", "2D动漫风格",
  "国风漫画风格", "水墨动画风格", "自定义参考图风格",
];

export default function StoryboardPage() {
  const [novels, setNovels] = useState<Novel[]>([]);
  const [projects, setProjects] = useState<Project[]>([]);
  const [episodes, setEpisodes] = useState<Episode[]>([]);
  const [shots, setShots] = useState<Shot[]>([]);
  const [assets, setAssets] = useState<VisualAsset[]>([]);
  const [novelId, setNovelId] = useState("");
  const [projectId, setProjectId] = useState<number | null>(null);
  const [episodeId, setEpisodeId] = useState<number | null>(null);
  const [projectName, setProjectName] = useState("");
  const [style, setStyle] = useState(styles[0]);
  const [message, setMessage] = useState("");
  const [busy, setBusy] = useState(false);

  const [shotForm, setShotForm] = useState({
    scene_name: "", shot_type: "中景", camera_movement: "固定镜头",
    duration_seconds: 5, visual_description: "", character_actions: "",
    dialogue: "", sound_effects: "", image_prompt: "",
  });

  const panel = "rounded-2xl border border-purple-300/20 bg-slate-950/65 p-5 shadow-xl shadow-purple-950/20";
  const field = "w-full rounded-xl border border-purple-300/20 bg-slate-900/80 px-3 py-2.5 text-sm text-white outline-none focus:border-purple-400";
  const button = "rounded-xl border border-purple-300/30 bg-purple-600/80 px-4 py-2 text-sm font-medium text-white transition hover:bg-purple-500 disabled:opacity-50";

  async function request(path: string, options?: RequestInit) {
    const response = await fetch(`${API}${path}`, options);
    const result = await response.json();
    if (!response.ok) throw new Error(result.detail || "请求失败");
    return result;
  }

  async function loadBase() {
    try {
      const [n, p, a] = await Promise.all([
        request("/api/story/novels"),
        request("/api/story/projects"),
        request("/api/story/assets"),
      ]);
      setNovels(n);
      setProjects(p);
      setAssets(a);
    } catch (error) {
      setMessage(error instanceof Error ? error.message : "读取数据失败");
    }
  }

  async function loadProject(id: number) {
    const result = await request(`/api/story/projects/${id}`);
    setProjectId(id);
    setEpisodes(result.episodes);
    setEpisodeId(null);
    setShots([]);
  }

  async function loadShots(id: number) {
    const result = await request(`/api/story/episodes/${id}/shots`);
    setShots(result);
    setEpisodeId(id);
  }

  useEffect(() => { loadBase(); }, []);

  async function createProject() {
    if (!novelId) return setMessage("请先选择小说");
    setBusy(true);
    try {
      const result = await request("/api/story/projects", {
        method: "POST",
        headers: { "Content-Type": "application/json" },
        body: JSON.stringify({
          novel_id: Number(novelId),
          name: projectName || "小说漫剧改编项目",
          adaptation_style: style,
          target_duration: 120,
          min_duration: 90,
          max_duration: 150,
        }),
      });
      setMessage("改编项目已创建");
      await loadBase();
      await loadProject(result.project.id);
    } catch (error) {
      setMessage(error instanceof Error ? error.message : "创建失败");
    } finally {
      setBusy(false);
    }
  }

  async function planEpisodes() {
    if (!projectId) return setMessage("请先选择剧本项目");
    setBusy(true);
    try {
      const result = await request(`/api/story/projects/${projectId}/plan`, { method: "POST" });
      setEpisodes(result.episodes);
      setMessage(result.message);
    } catch (error) {
      setMessage(error instanceof Error ? error.message : "分集规划失败");
    } finally {
      setBusy(false);
    }
  }

  async function updateEpisode(episode: Episode, patch: Partial<Episode>) {
    try {
      await request(`/api/story/episodes/${episode.id}`, {
        method: "PUT",
        headers: { "Content-Type": "application/json" },
        body: JSON.stringify(patch),
      });
      if (projectId) await loadProject(projectId);
      setMessage("分集信息已保存");
    } catch (error) {
      setMessage(error instanceof Error ? error.message : "保存失败");
    }
  }

  async function splitEpisode(id: number) {
    try {
      await request(`/api/story/episodes/${id}/split`, { method: "POST" });
      if (projectId) await loadProject(projectId);
      setMessage("分集已拆分");
    } catch (error) {
      setMessage(error instanceof Error ? error.message : "拆分失败");
    }
  }

  async function mergeEpisodes() {
    if (!projectId || episodes.length < 2) return;
    const ids = episodes.slice(0, 2).map(x => x.id);
    try {
      await request(`/api/story/projects/${projectId}/merge`, {
        method: "POST",
        headers: { "Content-Type": "application/json" },
        body: JSON.stringify({ episode_ids: ids, title: "合并分集" }),
      });
      await loadProject(projectId);
      setMessage("前两集已合并");
    } catch (error) {
      setMessage(error instanceof Error ? error.message : "合并失败");
    }
  }

  async function moveEpisode(index: number, direction: number) {
    if (!projectId) return;
    const next = [...episodes];
    const target = index + direction;
    if (target < 0 || target >= next.length) return;
    [next[index], next[target]] = [next[target], next[index]];
    try {
      await request(`/api/story/projects/${projectId}/reorder`, {
        method: "POST",
        headers: { "Content-Type": "application/json" },
        body: JSON.stringify({ episode_ids: next.map(x => x.id) }),
      });
      await loadProject(projectId);
    } catch (error) {
      setMessage(error instanceof Error ? error.message : "排序失败");
    }
  }

  async function addShot() {
    if (!episodeId) return setMessage("请先选择分集");
    try {
      await request(`/api/story/episodes/${episodeId}/shots`, {
        method: "POST",
        headers: { "Content-Type": "application/json" },
        body: JSON.stringify({
          ...shotForm,
          shot_number: shots.length + 1,
          sort_order: shots.length + 1,
        }),
      });
      await loadShots(episodeId);
      setMessage("分镜镜头已保存");
      setShotForm({
        scene_name: "", shot_type: "中景", camera_movement: "固定镜头",
        duration_seconds: 5, visual_description: "", character_actions: "",
        dialogue: "", sound_effects: "", image_prompt: "",
      });
    } catch (error) {
      setMessage(error instanceof Error ? error.message : "创建分镜失败");
    }
  }

  async function deleteShot(id: number) {
    try {
      await request(`/api/story/shots/${id}`, { method: "DELETE" });
      if (episodeId) await loadShots(episodeId);
    } catch (error) {
      setMessage(error instanceof Error ? error.message : "删除镜头失败");
    }
  }

  async function linkAsset(shot: Shot, assetId: number) {
    try {
      await request(`/api/story/shots/${shot.id}/assets`, {
        method: "POST",
        headers: { "Content-Type": "application/json" },
        body: JSON.stringify({ asset_id: assetId, role: "visual" }),
      });
      setMessage(`已将素材关联到镜头 #${shot.shot_number}`);
    } catch (error) {
      setMessage(error instanceof Error ? error.message : "素材关联失败");
    }
  }

  return (
    <main className="min-h-screen space-y-6 p-6 text-white">
      <header>
        <div className="text-sm text-purple-300">AI COMIC STUDIO / STORYBOARD</div>
        <h1 className="mt-2 text-3xl font-bold">剧本改编与智能分镜中心</h1>
        <p className="mt-2 text-sm text-slate-300">小说 → 分集规划 → 影视剧本 → 镜头分镜 → 视觉素材匹配</p>
      </header>

      {message && <div className="rounded-xl border border-purple-300/20 bg-purple-950/50 p-3 text-sm text-purple-100">{message}</div>}

      <section className={panel}>
        <h2 className="mb-4 text-xl font-semibold">创建小说改编项目</h2>
        <div className="grid gap-3 md:grid-cols-2">
          <select className={field} value={novelId} onChange={e => setNovelId(e.target.value)}>
            <option value="">选择已导入的小说</option>
            {novels.map(n => <option key={n.id} value={n.id}>{n.title || n.name || `小说 #${n.id}`}</option>)}
          </select>
          <input className={field} value={projectName} onChange={e => setProjectName(e.target.value)} placeholder="改编项目名称" />
          <select className={field} value={style} onChange={e => setStyle(e.target.value)}>
            {styles.map(s => <option key={s}>{s}</option>)}
          </select>
          <div className="rounded-xl border border-purple-300/15 bg-slate-900/70 p-3 text-sm text-slate-300">
            单集目标 120 秒；允许 90–150 秒。每章初步规划为 1 集，超长章节可拆为 2 集。
          </div>
        </div>
        <button className={`${button} mt-4`} disabled={busy} onClick={createProject}>创建改编项目</button>
      </section>

      <section className={panel}>
        <div className="mb-4 flex flex-wrap items-center justify-between gap-3">
          <h2 className="text-xl font-semibold">改编项目与自动分集</h2>
          <select className={`${field} max-w-sm`} value={projectId ?? ""} onChange={e => e.target.value && loadProject(Number(e.target.value))}>
            <option value="">选择已有改编项目</option>
            {projects.map(p => <option key={p.id} value={p.id}>{p.name}（#{p.id}）</option>)}
          </select>
        </div>
        <div className="flex flex-wrap gap-2">
          <button className={button} disabled={!projectId || busy} onClick={planEpisodes}>生成初步分集计划</button>
          <button className="rounded-xl border border-sky-300/30 bg-sky-800/60 px-4 py-2 text-sm" disabled={episodes.length < 2} onClick={mergeEpisodes}>合并前两集</button>
        </div>
        <p className="mt-2 text-xs text-slate-400">当前自动分集按章节长度进行初步规划；AI剧情理解、剧本改编将在后续 AI 编排阶段接入。</p>
        <div className="mt-4 space-y-3">
          {episodes.map((ep, index) => (
            <article key={ep.id} className={`rounded-xl border p-4 ${episodeId === ep.id ? "border-purple-400 bg-purple-950/40" : "border-purple-300/15 bg-slate-900/70"}`}>
              <div className="flex flex-wrap items-center justify-between gap-2">
                <button className="text-left font-semibold hover:text-purple-300" onClick={() => loadShots(ep.id)}>
                  第 {ep.episode_number} 集：{ep.title}
                </button>
                <span className="text-xs text-slate-400">{ep.duration_seconds} 秒 · {ep.status}</span>
              </div>
              <p className="mt-2 line-clamp-2 text-xs text-slate-400">原著内容：{ep.source_text?.slice(0, 180) || "暂无内容"}</p>
              <div className="mt-3 flex flex-wrap gap-2">
                <button className="rounded-lg border border-purple-300/20 px-3 py-1.5 text-xs" onClick={() => loadShots(ep.id)}>打开分镜</button>
                <button className="rounded-lg border border-purple-300/20 px-3 py-1.5 text-xs" onClick={() => splitEpisode(ep.id)}>拆分本集</button>
                <button className="rounded-lg border border-purple-300/20 px-3 py-1.5 text-xs" onClick={() => moveEpisode(index, -1)}>上移</button>
                <button className="rounded-lg border border-purple-300/20 px-3 py-1.5 text-xs" onClick={() => moveEpisode(index, 1)}>下移</button>
                <button className="rounded-lg border border-purple-300/20 px-3 py-1.5 text-xs" onClick={() => updateEpisode(ep, { duration_seconds: Math.min(150, ep.duration_seconds + 10) })}>时长 +10 秒</button>
              </div>
            </article>
          ))}
          {episodes.length === 0 && <p className="text-sm text-slate-400">选择或创建项目后，生成分集计划。</p>}
        </div>
      </section>

      <section className={panel}>
        <h2 className="mb-4 text-xl font-semibold">分镜镜头编辑器</h2>
        {!episodeId ? (
          <p className="text-sm text-slate-400">请先在上方选择一集，进入镜头编辑。</p>
        ) : (
          <>
            <div className="grid gap-3 md:grid-cols-2">
              <input className={field} placeholder="场景名称" value={shotForm.scene_name} onChange={e => setShotForm({ ...shotForm, scene_name: e.target.value })} />
              <select className={field} value={shotForm.shot_type} onChange={e => setShotForm({ ...shotForm, shot_type: e.target.value })}>
                {["远景", "全景", "中景", "近景", "特写", "大特写", "俯拍", "仰拍", "主观镜头"].map(x => <option key={x}>{x}</option>)}
              </select>
              <select className={field} value={shotForm.camera_movement} onChange={e => setShotForm({ ...shotForm, camera_movement: e.target.value })}>
                {["固定镜头", "推进", "拉远", "横移", "跟拍", "环绕", "摇镜头", "升降", "手持"].map(x => <option key={x}>{x}</option>)}
              </select>
              <input className={field} type="number" min="1" max="120" value={shotForm.duration_seconds} onChange={e => setShotForm({ ...shotForm, duration_seconds: Number(e.target.value) })} placeholder="镜头时长（秒）" />
              <textarea className={`${field} md:col-span-2`} rows={2} placeholder="画面内容、构图、光线、环境" value={shotForm.visual_description} onChange={e => setShotForm({ ...shotForm, visual_description: e.target.value })} />
              <textarea className={field} rows={2} placeholder="人物动作、表情、走位" value={shotForm.character_actions} onChange={e => setShotForm({ ...shotForm, character_actions: e.target.value })} />
              <textarea className={field} rows={2} placeholder="对白 / 旁白" value={shotForm.dialogue} onChange={e => setShotForm({ ...shotForm, dialogue: e.target.value })} />
              <textarea className={field} rows={2} placeholder="音效与环境声" value={shotForm.sound_effects} onChange={e => setShotForm({ ...shotForm, sound_effects: e.target.value })} />
              <textarea className={`${field} md:col-span-2`} rows={3} placeholder="供图片或视频模型使用的镜头提示词" value={shotForm.image_prompt} onChange={e => setShotForm({ ...shotForm, image_prompt: e.target.value })} />
            </div>
            <button className={`${button} mt-3`} onClick={addShot}>保存分镜镜头</button>
          </>
        )}
        <div className="mt-5 space-y-3">
          {shots.map(shot => (
            <article key={shot.id} className="rounded-xl border border-purple-300/15 bg-slate-900/70 p-4">
              <div className="flex items-center justify-between gap-2">
                <h3 className="font-semibold">镜头 {shot.shot_number} · {shot.shot_type} · {shot.camera_movement}</h3>
                <button className="text-xs text-red-300" onClick={() => deleteShot(shot.id)}>删除</button>
              </div>
              <p className="mt-2 text-sm text-slate-300">{shot.visual_description || "尚未填写画面描述"}</p>
              <p className="mt-1 text-xs text-slate-400">场景：{shot.scene_name || "未指定"} · 时长：{shot.duration_seconds} 秒</p>
              {shot.dialogue && <p className="mt-2 text-sm text-purple-200">对白：{shot.dialogue}</p>}
              <div className="mt-3 flex flex-wrap items-center gap-2">
                <span className="text-xs text-slate-400">关联视觉素材：</span>
                <select className="max-w-xs rounded-lg border border-purple-300/20 bg-slate-950 px-2 py-1 text-xs" defaultValue="" onChange={e => e.target.value && linkAsset(shot, Number(e.target.value))}>
                  <option value="">选择人物/场景/道具</option>
                  {assets.map(a => <option key={a.id} value={a.id}>{a.name}（{a.category}）</option>)}
                </select>
              </div>
            </article>
          ))}
          {episodeId && shots.length === 0 && <p className="text-sm text-slate-400">当前分集还没有分镜镜头。</p>}
        </div>
      </section>
    </main>
  );
}


========== 文件：apps\web\src\app\video-editor\page.tsx ==========
"use client";

import { useEffect, useState } from "react";

const API = process.env.NEXT_PUBLIC_API_BASE_URL || "http://127.0.0.1:8000";

type MediaFile = {
  id: number;
  name: string;
  category: string;
  path: string;
  size: number;
};

type EditJob = {
  id: number;
  title: string;
  provider: string;
  model: string;
  instruction: string;
  status: string;
  edit_plan: any;
  output_path: string;
  error: string;
};

export default function VideoEditorPage() {
  const [files, setFiles] = useState<MediaFile[]>([]);
  const [jobs, setJobs] = useState<EditJob[]>([]);
  const [provider, setProvider] = useState("deepseek");
  const [model, setModel] = useState("");
  const [instruction, setInstruction] = useState("");
  const [selected, setSelected] = useState<string[]>([]);
  const [confirmPaid, setConfirmPaid] = useState(false);
  const [subtitlePath, setSubtitlePath] = useState("");
  const [audioPath, setAudioPath] = useState("");
  const [message, setMessage] = useState("");
  const [busy, setBusy] = useState(false);

  async function loadAll() {
    try {
      const [fileResponse, jobResponse] = await Promise.all([
        fetch(`${API}/api/media/files?category=video`),
        fetch(`${API}/api/video-editor/jobs`)
      ]);
      if (fileResponse.ok) setFiles(await fileResponse.json());
      if (jobResponse.ok) setJobs(await jobResponse.json());
    } catch {
      setMessage("无法连接本地 API，请确认桌面软件服务已启动。");
    }
  }

  useEffect(() => { loadAll(); }, []);

  async function uploadFiles(list: FileList | null) {
    if (!list?.length) return;
    setBusy(true);
    setMessage("正在上传视频……");
    try {
      for (const file of Array.from(list)) {
        const form = new FormData();
        form.append("file", file);
        form.append("category", "video");
        const response = await fetch(`${API}/api/media/files/upload`, {
          method: "POST",
          body: form
        });
        const data = await response.json();
        if (!response.ok) throw new Error(data.detail || "上传失败");
      }
      setMessage("视频上传完成。");
      await loadAll();
    } catch (error) {
      setMessage(error instanceof Error ? error.message : "上传失败");
    } finally {
      setBusy(false);
    }
  }

  async function createPlan() {
    if (!selected.length) {
      setMessage("请先选择至少一个视频文件。");
      return;
    }
    setBusy(true);
    setMessage("正在调用 AI 分析剪辑要求……");
    try {
      const response = await fetch(`${API}/api/video-editor/jobs`, {
        method: "POST",
        headers: { "Content-Type": "application/json" },
        body: JSON.stringify({
          provider, model, instruction,
          source_paths: selected,
          confirm_paid: confirmPaid
        })
      });
      const data = await response.json();
      if (!response.ok) throw new Error(data.detail || "AI 分析失败");
      setMessage(`AI 剪辑方案已生成，任务编号：${data.id}`);
      await loadAll();
    } catch (error) {
      setMessage(error instanceof Error ? error.message : "AI 分析失败");
    } finally {
      setBusy(false);
    }
  }

  async function executeJob(id: number) {
    setBusy(true);
    setMessage("FFmpeg 正在执行视频剪辑，请等待……");
    try {
      const response = await fetch(`${API}/api/video-editor/jobs/${id}/execute`, {
        method: "POST",
        headers: { "Content-Type": "application/json" },
        body: JSON.stringify({
          subtitle_path: subtitlePath || null,
          audio_path: audioPath || null
        })
      });
      const data = await response.json();
      if (!response.ok) throw new Error(data.detail || "剪辑执行失败");
      setMessage(`视频导出完成：${data.output_path}`);
      await loadAll();
    } catch (error) {
      setMessage(error instanceof Error ? error.message : "剪辑失败");
      await loadAll();
    } finally {
      setBusy(false);
    }
  }

  function toggleFile(path: string) {
    setSelected(current =>
      current.includes(path)
        ? current.filter(x => x !== path)
        : [...current, path]
    );
  }

  function formatSize(size: number) {
    return size >= 1024 ** 3
      ? `${(size / 1024 ** 3).toFixed(2)} GB`
      : `${(size / 1024 ** 2).toFixed(1)} MB`;
  }

  return (
    <main className="min-h-screen p-8 text-white">
      <div className="mx-auto max-w-7xl space-y-7">
        <header>
          <h1 className="text-3xl font-bold">AI 视频剪辑中心</h1>
          <p className="mt-2 text-white/60">
            用文字描述剪辑要求，让 AI 规划，再由本地 FFmpeg 执行
          </p>
        </header>

        {message && (
          <div className="rounded-xl border border-purple-400/30 bg-purple-500/10 p-4">
            {message}
          </div>
        )}

        <section className="grid gap-6 lg:grid-cols-2">
          <div className="rounded-2xl border border-purple-300/20 bg-slate-950/65 p-6">
            <h2 className="mb-4 text-xl font-semibold">第一步：导入原视频</h2>
            <label className="block cursor-pointer rounded-xl border border-dashed border-purple-300/40 p-8 text-center hover:bg-purple-500/10">
              <span className="text-lg">选择视频文件</span>
              <p className="mt-2 text-sm text-white/50">支持多个视频，单文件上限 2GB</p>
              <input type="file" accept="video/*" multiple className="hidden"
                onChange={e => uploadFiles(e.target.files)} />
            </label>

            <h3 className="mb-3 mt-6 font-semibold">本地视频素材</h3>
            <div className="max-h-72 space-y-2 overflow-auto">
              {files.map(file => (
                <label key={file.id}
                  className="flex cursor-pointer items-center gap-3 rounded-lg border border-white/10 p-3">
                  <input type="checkbox"
                    checked={selected.includes(file.path)}
                    onChange={() => toggleFile(file.path)} />
                  <span className="min-w-0 flex-1 truncate">{file.name}</span>
                  <span className="text-xs text-white/50">{formatSize(file.size)}</span>
                </label>
              ))}
              {files.length === 0 && <p className="text-sm text-white/40">还没有导入视频</p>}
            </div>
          </div>

          <div className="rounded-2xl border border-purple-300/20 bg-slate-950/65 p-6">
            <h2 className="mb-4 text-xl font-semibold">第二步：描述剪辑要求</h2>
            <label className="mb-2 block text-sm">AI 服务商 ID</label>
            <input value={provider} onChange={e => setProvider(e.target.value)}
              className="mb-4 w-full rounded-lg border border-white/15 bg-black/30 p-3" />

            <label className="mb-2 block text-sm">AI 模型名称</label>
            <input value={model} onChange={e => setModel(e.target.value)}
              placeholder="例如 deepseek-chat"
              className="mb-4 w-full rounded-lg border border-white/15 bg-black/30 p-3" />

            <label className="mb-2 block text-sm">剪辑要求</label>
            <textarea value={instruction} onChange={e => setInstruction(e.target.value)}
              rows={7}
              placeholder="例如：保留人物说话和动作的部分，删除等待和空镜头。开头使用最精彩的画面，按故事发展顺序拼接，输出横屏 1080P 视频。"
              className="mb-4 w-full rounded-lg border border-white/15 bg-black/30 p-3" />

            <label className="mb-4 flex items-center gap-2 text-sm">
              <input type="checkbox" checked={confirmPaid}
                onChange={e => setConfirmPaid(e.target.checked)} />
              我确认本次 AI 分析可能产生 API 费用
            </label>

            <button disabled={busy || !model.trim() || !instruction.trim()}
              onClick={createPlan}
              className="rounded-xl bg-purple-600 px-6 py-3 font-semibold disabled:opacity-40">
              AI 分析并生成剪辑方案
            </button>
          </div>
        </section>

        <section className="rounded-2xl border border-purple-300/20 bg-slate-950/65 p-6">
          <h2 className="mb-4 text-xl font-semibold">第三步：可选字幕与音轨</h2>
          <div className="grid gap-4 md:grid-cols-2">
            <div>
              <label className="mb-2 block text-sm">字幕文件路径（可选）</label>
              <input value={subtitlePath} onChange={e => setSubtitlePath(e.target.value)}
                placeholder="storage/subtitles/xxx.srt 的完整路径"
                className="w-full rounded-lg border border-white/15 bg-black/30 p-3" />
            </div>
            <div>
              <label className="mb-2 block text-sm">替换音轨路径（可选）</label>
              <input value={audioPath} onChange={e => setAudioPath(e.target.value)}
                placeholder="storage/audio/xxx.mp3 的完整路径"
                className="w-full rounded-lg border border-white/15 bg-black/30 p-3" />
            </div>
          </div>
          <p className="mt-3 text-xs text-white/45">
            当前选择音轨会替换原视频音轨；不填写则保留视频原音轨。
          </p>
        </section>

        <section className="rounded-2xl border border-purple-300/20 bg-slate-950/65 p-6">
          <div className="mb-4 flex items-center justify-between">
            <h2 className="text-xl font-semibold">剪辑任务与导出</h2>
            <button onClick={loadAll} className="rounded-lg border border-white/20 px-4 py-2">
              刷新
            </button>
          </div>

          <div className="space-y-4">
            {jobs.map(job => (
              <div key={job.id} className="rounded-xl border border-white/10 bg-black/20 p-4">
                <div className="flex flex-wrap items-center justify-between gap-3">
                  <div>
                    <h3 className="font-semibold">{job.title} · #{job.id}</h3>
                    <p className="mt-1 text-sm text-white/50">
                      {job.provider} / {job.model} · {job.status}
                    </p>
                  </div>
                  {job.status === "planned" && (
                    <button disabled={busy} onClick={() => executeJob(job.id)}
                      className="rounded-lg bg-indigo-600 px-5 py-2 disabled:opacity-40">
                      执行剪辑并导出
                    </button>
                  )}
                </div>

                {job.edit_plan && (
                  <div className="mt-4 space-y-2 text-sm">
                    <p className="text-white/70">{job.edit_plan.summary}</p>
                    <p>片段数量：{job.edit_plan.segments?.length || 0}</p>
                    <div className="max-h-48 space-y-1 overflow-auto text-white/50">
                      {job.edit_plan.segments?.map((segment: any, index: number) => (
                        <p key={index}>
                          {index + 1}. {segment.start}s – {segment.end}s：
                          {segment.description}
                        </p>
                      ))}
                    </div>
                  </div>
                )}

                {job.output_path && (
                  <p className="mt-3 break-all text-sm text-green-300">
                    导出文件：{job.output_path}
                  </p>
                )}
                {job.error && <p className="mt-3 text-sm text-red-300">{job.error}</p>}
              </div>
            ))}
            {jobs.length === 0 && <p className="text-white/40">暂无剪辑任务</p>}
          </div>
        </section>
      </div>
    </main>
  );
}


========== 文件：apps\web\src\app\visual-studio\page.tsx ==========
"use client";

import { useEffect, useState } from "react";

const API = process.env.NEXT_PUBLIC_API_BASE_URL || "http://127.0.0.1:8000";

type Asset = {
  id: number;
  category: string;
  name: string;
  description: string;
  appearance: string;
  clothing: string;
  personality: string;
  consistency_prompt: string;
  style: string;
  image_path: string;
  confirmed: boolean;
};

type Task = {
  id: number;
  task_type: string;
  prompt: string;
  status: string;
  error: string;
  result_path: string;
};

const categories = [
  { key: "characters", label: "人物角色" },
  { key: "scenes", label: "场景环境" },
  { key: "props", label: "物品道具" },
];

const styles = [
  "3D动漫电影风格",
  "真人电影风格",
  "2D动漫风格",
  "国风漫画风格",
  "水墨动画风格",
  "自定义参考图风格",
];

export default function VisualStudioPage() {
  const [category, setCategory] = useState("characters");
  const [assets, setAssets] = useState<Asset[]>([]);
  const [tasks, setTasks] = useState<Task[]>([]);
  const [name, setName] = useState("");
  const [description, setDescription] = useState("");
  const [appearance, setAppearance] = useState("");
  const [clothing, setClothing] = useState("");
  const [personality, setPersonality] = useState("");
  const [consistency, setConsistency] = useState("");
  const [style, setStyle] = useState(styles[0]);
  const [prompt, setPrompt] = useState("");
  const [selectedAsset, setSelectedAsset] = useState<number | null>(null);
  const [confirmPaid, setConfirmPaid] = useState(false);
  const [message, setMessage] = useState("");
  const [busy, setBusy] = useState(false);

  async function loadAssets() {
    try {
      const response = await fetch(`${API}/api/visual/assets?category=${category}`);
      if (response.ok) setAssets(await response.json());
    } catch {}
  }

  async function loadTasks() {
    try {
      const response = await fetch(`${API}/api/visual/tasks`);
      if (response.ok) setTasks(await response.json());
    } catch {}
  }

  useEffect(() => {
    loadAssets();
  }, [category]);

  useEffect(() => {
    loadTasks();
  }, []);

  async function saveAsset() {
    if (!name.trim()) {
      setMessage("请先填写素材名称");
      return;
    }
    setBusy(true);
    try {
      const response = await fetch(`${API}/api/visual/assets`, {
        method: "POST",
        headers: { "Content-Type": "application/json" },
        body: JSON.stringify({
          category, name, description, appearance, clothing,
          personality, consistency_prompt: consistency, style, data: {},
        }),
      });
      const result = await response.json();
      if (!response.ok) throw new Error(result.detail || "保存失败");
      setMessage("素材设定已保存");
      setName("");
      setDescription("");
      setAppearance("");
      setClothing("");
      setPersonality("");
      setConsistency("");
      await loadAssets();
    } catch (error) {
      setMessage(error instanceof Error ? error.message : "保存失败");
    } finally {
      setBusy(false);
    }
  }

  function buildPrompt() {
    const selected = assets.find((item) => item.id === selectedAsset);
    const base = selected
      ? `${selected.name}。${selected.description}。外貌：${selected.appearance}。服装：${selected.clothing}。角色固定特征：${selected.consistency_prompt}。`
      : "";
    const full = `${base}${prompt}。风格：${style}。高质量电影级画面，精细材质，清晰细节，统一角色设计。`;
    setPrompt(full);
  }

  async function createTask() {
    if (!prompt.trim()) {
      setMessage("请填写图片生成提示词");
      return;
    }
    setBusy(true);
    try {
      const response = await fetch(`${API}/api/visual/tasks`, {
        method: "POST",
        headers: { "Content-Type": "application/json" },
        body: JSON.stringify({
          asset_id: selectedAsset,
          provider: "doubao",
          task_type: category,
          prompt,
          size: "2K",
          confirm_paid: confirmPaid,
        }),
      });
      const result = await response.json();
      if (!response.ok) throw new Error(result.detail || "创建任务失败");
      setMessage(`图片任务 #${result.task_id} 已加入队列`);
      await loadTasks();
    } catch (error) {
      setMessage(error instanceof Error ? error.message : "创建任务失败");
    } finally {
      setBusy(false);
    }
  }

  async function runTask(id: number) {
    setBusy(true);
    try {
      const response = await fetch(
        `${API}/api/visual/tasks/${id}/run?confirm_paid=${confirmPaid}`,
        { method: "POST" }
      );
      const result = await response.json();
      if (!response.ok) throw new Error(result.detail || "生成失败");
      setMessage(`图片任务 #${id} 已完成`);
      await loadTasks();
      await loadAssets();
    } catch (error) {
      setMessage(error instanceof Error ? error.message : "生成失败");
      await loadTasks();
    } finally {
      setBusy(false);
    }
  }

  async function uploadReference(file?: File) {
    if (!file) return;
    const form = new FormData();
    form.append("file", file);
    if (selectedAsset) form.append("asset_id", String(selectedAsset));
    try {
      const response = await fetch(`${API}/api/visual/references`, {
        method: "POST",
        body: form,
      });
      const result = await response.json();
      if (!response.ok) throw new Error(result.detail || "上传失败");
      setMessage("参考图片已上传");
    } catch (error) {
      setMessage(error instanceof Error ? error.message : "上传失败");
    }
  }

  const panel = "rounded-2xl border border-purple-300/20 bg-slate-950/65 p-5 shadow-xl shadow-purple-950/20";
  const field = "w-full rounded-xl border border-purple-300/20 bg-slate-900/80 px-3 py-2.5 text-sm text-white outline-none focus:border-purple-400";
  const button = "rounded-xl border border-purple-300/30 bg-purple-600/80 px-4 py-2 text-sm font-medium text-white transition hover:bg-purple-500 disabled:opacity-50";

  return (
    <main className="min-h-screen space-y-6 p-6 text-white">
      <header>
        <div className="text-sm text-purple-300">AI COMIC STUDIO / VISUAL LAB</div>
        <h1 className="mt-2 text-3xl font-bold">AI视觉素材生产中心</h1>
        <p className="mt-2 text-sm text-slate-300">
          统一管理人物、场景、道具、角色一致性和 AI 图片生成任务。
        </p>
      </header>

      {message && (
        <div className="rounded-xl border border-purple-300/20 bg-purple-950/50 p-3 text-sm text-purple-100">
          {message}
        </div>
      )}

      <section className="grid gap-3 md:grid-cols-3">
        {categories.map((item) => (
          <button
            key={item.key}
            onClick={() => setCategory(item.key)}
            className={`rounded-xl border p-4 text-left transition ${
              category === item.key
                ? "border-purple-400 bg-purple-700/40"
                : "border-purple-300/20 bg-slate-950/60 hover:bg-purple-950/40"
            }`}
          >
            <div className="text-lg font-semibold">{item.label}</div>
            <div className="mt-1 text-xs text-slate-400">
              {item.key === "characters" ? "人物设定、服装、三视图与一致性" :
               item.key === "scenes" ? "地点、环境、时间、氛围与场景图" :
               "物品外观、材质、尺寸与道具图"}
            </div>
          </button>
        ))}
      </section>

      <section className="grid gap-6 xl:grid-cols-2">
        <div className={panel}>
          <h2 className="mb-4 text-xl font-semibold">建立视觉素材设定</h2>
          <div className="space-y-3">
            <input className={field} placeholder="素材名称" value={name} onChange={(e) => setName(e.target.value)} />
            <textarea className={field} rows={2} placeholder="基础描述 / 故事背景" value={description} onChange={(e) => setDescription(e.target.value)} />
            <textarea className={field} rows={3} placeholder="外貌、外形、颜色、体型、材质等" value={appearance} onChange={(e) => setAppearance(e.target.value)} />
            <textarea className={field} rows={2} placeholder="服装、装备或表面细节（场景和道具可填写对应特征）" value={clothing} onChange={(e) => setClothing(e.target.value)} />
            <textarea className={field} rows={2} placeholder="性格、气质或视觉氛围" value={personality} onChange={(e) => setPersonality(e.target.value)} />
            <textarea className={field} rows={3} placeholder="固定特征：发型、脸型、颜色、标志物等，供后续保持一致" value={consistency} onChange={(e) => setConsistency(e.target.value)} />
            <select className={field} value={style} onChange={(e) => setStyle(e.target.value)}>
              {styles.map((item) => <option key={item}>{item}</option>)}
            </select>
            <button className={button} disabled={busy} onClick={saveAsset}>保存素材设定</button>
          </div>
        </div>

        <div className={panel}>
          <h2 className="mb-4 text-xl font-semibold">AI图片生成</h2>
          <div className="space-y-3">
            <label className="block text-sm text-slate-300">关联素材</label>
            <select
              className={field}
              value={selectedAsset ?? ""}
              onChange={(e) => setSelectedAsset(e.target.value ? Number(e.target.value) : null)}
            >
              <option value="">不关联素材</option>
              {assets.map((item) => <option key={item.id} value={item.id}>{item.name}</option>)}
            </select>
            <textarea
              className={field}
              rows={7}
              placeholder="填写图片生成要求，例如：角色正面、侧面、背面三视图，纯色背景，全身站立，三个人物视角保持同一比例……"
              value={prompt}
              onChange={(e) => setPrompt(e.target.value)}
            />
            <button className="rounded-xl border border-sky-300/30 bg-sky-700/60 px-4 py-2 text-sm text-white hover:bg-sky-600" onClick={buildPrompt}>
              根据素材设定组合提示词
            </button>
            <label className="flex items-center gap-2 text-sm text-slate-300">
              <input type="checkbox" checked={confirmPaid} onChange={(e) => setConfirmPaid(e.target.checked)} />
              我已了解本次图片生成可能产生费用，并确认执行
            </label>
            <div className="flex flex-wrap gap-2">
              <button className={button} disabled={busy} onClick={createTask}>创建图片任务</button>
              <label className="cursor-pointer rounded-xl border border-purple-300/30 px-4 py-2 text-sm hover:bg-purple-950/50">
                上传参考图片
                <input type="file" accept="image/*" className="hidden" onChange={(e) => uploadReference(e.target.files?.[0])} />
              </label>
            </div>
            <p className="text-xs leading-5 text-slate-400">
              图片生成只在你主动点击执行后调用。付费服务还需要 AI 管理中心开启付费授权并在本页确认。
            </p>
          </div>
        </div>
      </section>

      <section className={panel}>
        <div className="mb-4 flex items-center justify-between">
          <h2 className="text-xl font-semibold">当前素材库</h2>
          <button className={button} onClick={loadAssets}>刷新素材</button>
        </div>
        {assets.length === 0 ? (
          <p className="text-sm text-slate-400">当前分类还没有素材。</p>
        ) : (
          <div className="grid gap-3 md:grid-cols-2 xl:grid-cols-3">
            {assets.map((item) => (
              <article key={item.id} className="rounded-xl border border-purple-300/15 bg-slate-900/70 p-4">
                <div className="flex items-center justify-between gap-2">
                  <h3 className="font-semibold">{item.name}</h3>
                  <span className="text-xs text-slate-400">{item.confirmed ? "已确认" : "待确认"}</span>
                </div>
                <p className="mt-2 line-clamp-3 text-sm text-slate-400">{item.description || item.appearance}</p>
                <p className="mt-2 text-xs text-purple-300">{item.style}</p>
                {item.image_path && (
                  <p className="mt-2 break-all text-xs text-slate-500">图片已生成：{item.image_path}</p>
                )}
                <button
                  className="mt-3 rounded-lg border border-purple-300/20 px-3 py-1.5 text-xs hover:bg-purple-900/50"
                  onClick={() => {
                    setSelectedAsset(item.id);
                    setName(item.name);
                    setDescription(item.description || "");
                    setAppearance(item.appearance || "");
                    setClothing(item.clothing || "");
                    setPersonality(item.personality || "");
                    setConsistency(item.consistency_prompt || "");
                    setPrompt("");
                  }}
                >
                  使用此素材生成图片
                </button>
              </article>
            ))}
          </div>
        )}
      </section>

      <section className={panel}>
        <div className="mb-4 flex items-center justify-between">
          <h2 className="text-xl font-semibold">图片生成任务队列</h2>
          <button className={button} onClick={loadTasks}>刷新任务</button>
        </div>
        <div className="space-y-3">
          {tasks.length === 0 && <p className="text-sm text-slate-400">还没有图片生成任务。</p>}
          {tasks.map((task) => (
            <div key={task.id} className="flex flex-wrap items-center justify-between gap-3 rounded-xl border border-purple-300/15 bg-slate-900/70 p-3">
              <div className="min-w-0 flex-1">
                <div className="font-medium">任务 #{task.id} · {task.task_type}</div>
                <p className="mt-1 line-clamp-2 text-xs text-slate-400">{task.prompt}</p>
                {task.error && <p className="mt-1 text-xs text-red-300">{task.error}</p>}
              </div>
              <span className="text-xs text-purple-300">{task.status}</span>
              {task.status === "pending" && (
                <button className={button} disabled={busy} onClick={() => runTask(task.id)}>执行生成</button>
              )}
            </div>
          ))}
        </div>
      </section>
    </main>
  );
}


========== 文件：scripts\build-windows.ps1 ==========
$ErrorActionPreference = "Stop"

# Automatically locate the project root.
$Root = Split-Path -Parent $PSScriptRoot
$Api = Join-Path $Root "apps\api"
$Web = Join-Path $Root "apps\web"
$Desktop = Join-Path $Root "apps\desktop"
$Python = Join-Path $Api ".venv\Scripts\python.exe"

function Check-ExitCode {
    param([string]$Message)

    if ($LASTEXITCODE -ne 0) {
        throw "$Message. Exit code: $LASTEXITCODE"
    }
}

Write-Host "========== WINDOWS BUILD START =========="
Write-Host "Project root: $Root"

if (!(Test-Path $Python)) {
    throw "Python virtual environment not found."
}

if (!(Test-Path "$Desktop\node_modules\electron-builder")) {
    throw "electron-builder dependency not found."
}

if (!(Test-Path "$Web\node_modules")) {
    throw "Web node_modules not found."
}

Write-Host "[1/4] Checking PyInstaller..."

& $Python -m pip show pyinstaller *> $null

if ($LASTEXITCODE -ne 0) {
    & $Python -m pip install pyinstaller
    Check-ExitCode "PyInstaller installation failed"
}

Write-Host "[2/4] Building API executable..."

Set-Location $Api

New-Item -ItemType Directory -Path "$Api\dist" -Force | Out-Null
New-Item -ItemType Directory -Path "$Api\build\pyinstaller" -Force | Out-Null

$PyInstallerArgs = @(
    "-m", "PyInstaller",
    "--noconfirm",
    "--clean",
    "--onefile",
    "--name", "ai-comic-api",
    "--distpath", "$Api\dist",
    "--workpath", "$Api\build\pyinstaller",
    "--specpath", "$Api\build",
    "--paths", $Api,
    "--collect-submodules", "app",
    "--collect-all", "uvicorn",
    "--collect-all", "fastapi",
    "--collect-all", "starlette",
    "--collect-all", "sqlalchemy",
    "--collect-all", "pydantic",
    "--collect-all", "cryptography",
    "--collect-all", "multipart",
    "$Api\build_entry.py"
)

& $Python @PyInstallerArgs
Check-ExitCode "API packaging failed"

$ApiExe = "$Api\dist\ai-comic-api.exe"

if (!(Test-Path $ApiExe)) {
    throw "API executable was not generated."
}

Write-Host "API executable created."

Write-Host "[3/4] Building Next.js frontend..."

Set-Location $Web

npm run build
Check-ExitCode "Next.js build failed"

if (!(Test-Path "$Web\.next\standalone\server.js")) {
    throw "Next.js standalone server.js not found."
}

if (!(Test-Path "$Web\.next\static")) {
    throw "Next.js static directory not found."
}

Write-Host "Frontend standalone build created."

Write-Host "[4/4] Building Electron installer..."

Set-Location $Desktop

npm run build:desktop
Check-ExitCode "Electron installer build failed"

$Release = Join-Path $Desktop "release"

if (!(Test-Path $Release)) {
    throw "Release directory not found."
}

$Installers = @(Get-ChildItem $Release -File -Filter "*.exe")

if ($Installers.Count -eq 0) {
    throw "No installer EXE was generated."
}

Write-Host "========== BUILD SUCCESS =========="

foreach ($Installer in $Installers) {
    Write-Host $Installer.FullName
    Write-Host ("Size: {0} MB" -f [math]::Round($Installer.Length / 1MB, 2))
}

Set-Location $Root
